"""
Process Manager — управление процессами через React Flow
"""
import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from contextlib import contextmanager
import os

# Dynamic path - use environment variable or default to local processes.db
DB_PATH = Path(os.getenv("PROCESSES_DB_PATH", str(Path(__file__).parent / "processes.db")))

class ProcessManager:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = None):
        self.db_path = db_path or str(DB_PATH)
        self._init_db()
    
    def init_components_db():
        """Initialize components table"""
        # Ensure directory exists
        db_dir = Path(DB_PATH).parent
        db_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        cursor.execute('''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS components (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                type TEXT NOT NULL,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                tags TEXT,
                data TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP
            )
        ''')
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    @contextmanager
    def _get_connection(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        try:
            yield conn
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    def create(self, name: str, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], description: str = "") -> str:
        """Создаёт новый процесс"""
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO processes (id, name, description, nodes, edges, status)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, name, description, json.dumps(nodes), json.dumps(edges), 'stopped'))
        
        return process_id
    
    def get(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                return {
                    'id': row['id'],
                    'name': row['name'],
                    'description': row['description'],
                    'nodes': json.loads(row['nodes']),
                    'edges': json.loads(row['edges']),
                    'status': row['status'],
                    'created_at': row['created_at'],
                    'updated_at': row['updated_at'],
                    'last_run': row['last_run'],
                    'runs_count': row['runs_count']
                }
        return None
    
    def list_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, description, status, created_at, updated_at, runs_count
                FROM processes ORDER BY updated_at DESC
            """).fetchall()
            return [dict(row) for row in rows]
    
    def update(self, process_id: str, name: str = None, description: str = None, 
               nodes: List = None, edges: List = None):
        updates = []
        params = []
        
        if name:
            updates.append("name = ?")
            params.append(name)
        if description is not None:
            updates.append("description = ?")
            params.append(description)
        if nodes:
            updates.append("nodes = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(nodes))
        if edges:
            updates.append("edges = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(edges))
        
        if updates:
            updates.append("updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                conn.execute(f"UPDATE processes SET {', '.join(updates)} WHERE id = ?", params + [process_id])
    
    def delete(self, process_id: str):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
    
    def record_run(self, process_id: str, status: str, output: str = "", error: str = ""):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO process_runs (process_id, started_at, completed_at, status, output, error)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, datetime.now().isoformat(), datetime.now().isoformat(), status, output, error))
            
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE processes SET status = ?, last_run = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, runs_count = runs_count + 1
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (status, process_id))
    
    async def execute_node(self, node_id: str, node_config: Dict, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет один узел процесса"""
        from nodes import NodeRegistry        
        
        print(f"🔍 process_manager: node_config = {node_config}")
        node_type = node_config.get('data', {}).get('node_type', node_config.get('type'))
        if not node_type:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Node type not specified for {node_id}"}
        
        node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
        if not node_instance:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"}
        
        # Извлекаем конфигурацию узла
        config = node_config.get('data', {})
        # Убираем мета-поля
        for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color']:
            config.pop(key, None)
        
        # Добавляем ID узла в контекст
        if context is None:
            context = {}
        context['current_node_id'] = node_id
        
        print(f"🔧 Выполнение узла {node_id} (тип: {node_type})")
        
        try:
            result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
            return {
                "success": result.get("success", True),
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "result": result
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "error": str(e)
            }
    
    async def execute_process(self, process_id: str, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        print("🔵 ВЕРСИЯ 1")
        """Выполняет весь процесс по ID"""
        process = self.get(process_id)
        if not process:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Process not found"}
        
        nodes = process.get('nodes', [])
        edges = process.get('edges', [])
        
        # Строим граф зависимостей
        node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}
        children_map = {}
        for edge in edges:
            source = edge.get('source')
            target = edge.get('target')
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
        
        # Находим стартовые узлы (без входящих рёбер)
        all_targets = set(edge.get('target') for edge in edges)
        start_nodes = [node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] not in all_targets]
        
        if not start_nodes:
            start_nodes = [nodes[0]['id']] if nodes else []
        
        # Записываем запуск
        self.record_run(process_id, 'running')
        
        results = {}
        errors = []
        
        # Выполняем узлы в порядке
        for node_id in start_nodes:
            node = node_map.get(node_id)
            if node:
                result = await self.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
                results[node_id] = result
                
                if not result.get('success', False):
                    errors.append(result)
                    break
        
        status = 'failed' if errors else 'completed'
        self.record_run(process_id, status)
        
        return {
            "success": len(errors) == 0,
            "process_id": process_id,
            "results": results,
            "errors": errors if errors else None
        }

# Синглтон
_manager = None

def get_manager():
    global _manager
    if _manager is None:
        _manager = ProcessManager()
    return _manager
    
    print("🟢 ВЕРСИЯ 2")
    async def execute_process(self, process_id: str, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет весь процесс с поддержкой condition узлов"""
        process = self.get(process_id)
        if not process:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Process not found"}
        
        nodes = process.get('nodes', [])
        edges = process.get('edges', [])
        
        # Строим граф зависимостей
        node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}
        children_map = {}
        for edge in edges:
            source = edge.get('source')
            target = edge.get('target')
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
        
        # Находим стартовые узлы
        all_targets = set(edge.get('target') for edge in edges)
        start_nodes = [node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] not in all_targets]
        
        if not start_nodes and nodes:
            start_nodes = [nodes[0]['id']]
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'running')
        
        results = {}
        queue = list(start_nodes)
        visited = set()
        
        while queue:
            node_id = queue.pop(0)
            if node_id in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(node_id)
            
            node = node_map.get(node_id)
            if not node:
                continue
            
            result = await self.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
            results[node_id] = result
            
            if result.get('success', False):
                # Проверяем, есть ли следующий узел
                next_nodes = children_map.get(node_id, [])
                
                # Если текущий узел - condition, используем его next_node
                node_type = node.get('data', {}).get('node_type')
                if node_type == 'condition' and result.get('result', {}).get('next_node'):
                    next_nodes = [result['result']['next_node']]
                
                for next_id in next_nodes:
                    if next_id not in visited:
                        queue.append(next_id)
            else:
                # Если узел failed, останавливаем выполнение
                self.record_run(process_id, 'failed')
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "process_id": process_id,
                    "results": results,
                    "error": f"Node {node_id} failed"
                }
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'completed')
        return {
            "success": True,
            "process_id": process_id,
            "results": results
        }
        print("🔴 ВЕРСИЯ 3")

    async def execute_process(self, process_id: str, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет весь процесс с поддержкой condition узлов"""
        print(f"🔍 [execute_process] process_id={process_id}, context={context}")
        process = self.get(process_id)
        if not process:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Process not found"}
        
        nodes = process.get('nodes', [])
        edges = process.get('edges', [])
        
        # Строим граф зависимостей
        node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}
        children_map = {}
        for edge in edges:
            source = edge.get('source')
            target = edge.get('target')
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
        
        print(f"📊 Children map: {children_map}")
        
        # Находим стартовые узлы (без входящих рёбер)
        all_targets = set(edge.get('target') for edge in edges)
        start_nodes = [node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] not in all_targets]
        
        print(f"📍 Start nodes: {start_nodes}")
        
        if not start_nodes and nodes:
            start_nodes = [nodes[0]['id']]
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'running')
        
        results = {}
        queue = list(start_nodes)
        visited = set()
        
        while queue:
            node_id = queue.pop(0)
            print(f"🔄 Processing node: {node_id}")
            
            if node_id in visited:
                print(f"  ⏭️ Already visited: {node_id}")
                continue
            visited.add(node_id)
            
            node = node_map.get(node_id)
            if not node:
                print(f"  ❌ Node not found: {node_id}")
                continue
            
            result = await self.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
            results[node_id] = result
            print(f"  📊 Result: success={result.get('success')}")
            
            if result.get('success', False):
                # Получаем следующий узел
                next_nodes = children_map.get(node_id, [])
                print(f"  ➡️ Next nodes from map: {next_nodes}")
                
                # Если текущий узел - condition, используем его next_node
                node_type = node.get('data', {}).get('node_type')
                if node_type == 'condition' and result.get('result', {}).get('next_node'):
                    next_nodes = [result['result']['next_node']]
                    print(f"  🔀 Condition branch: {next_nodes}")
                
                for next_id in next_nodes:
                    if next_id not in visited:
                        print(f"  📌 Adding to queue: {next_id}")
                        queue.append(next_id)
            else:
                print(f"  ❌ Node failed, stopping execution")
                self.record_run(process_id, 'failed')
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "process_id": process_id,
                    "results": results,
                    "error": f"Node {node_id} failed"
                }
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'completed')
        return {
            "success": True,
            "process_id": process_id,
            "results": results
        }

    def substitute_vars(self, node: Dict, variables: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Подстановка переменных в конфигурацию узла"""
        import re
        
        def replace_var(value):
            if isinstance(value, str):
                def replacer(match):
                    var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
                    return str(variables.get(var_name, match.group(0)))
                return re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replacer, value)
            elif isinstance(value, dict):
                return {k: replace_var(v) for k, v in value.items()}
            elif isinstance(value, list):
                return [replace_var(item) for item in value]
            return value
        
        if "data" in node:
            node["data"] = replace_var(node["data"])
        return node

```python
"""
Process Manager — управление процессами через React Flow
"""
import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from contextlib import contextmanager
import os

# Dynamic path - use environment variable or default to local processes.db
DB_PATH = Path(os.getenv("PROCESSES_DB_PATH", str(Path(__file__).parent / "processes.db")))

class ProcessManager:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = None):
        self.db_path = db_path or str(DB_PATH)
        self._init_db()
    
    def init_components_db():
        """Initialize components table"""
        # Ensure directory exists
        db_dir = Path(DB_PATH).parent
        db_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        cursor.execute('''
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS components (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                type TEXT NOT NULL,
                name TEXT NOT NULL,
                description TEXT,
                tags TEXT,
                data TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMP
            )
        ''')
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    @contextmanager
    def _get_connection(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        try:
            yield conn
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    def create(self, name: str, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], description: str = "") -> str:
        """Создаёт новый процесс"""
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO processes (id, name, description, nodes, edges, status)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, name, description, json.dumps(nodes), json.dumps(edges), 'stopped'))
        
        return process_id
    
    def get(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                return {
                    'id': row['id'],
                    'name': row['name'],
                    'description': row['description'],
                    'nodes': json.loads(row['nodes']),
                    'edges': json.loads(row['edges']),
                    'status': row['status'],
                    'created_at': row['created_at'],
                    'updated_at': row['updated_at'],
                    'last_run': row['last_run'],
                    'runs_count': row['runs_count']
                }
        return None
    
    def list_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, description, status, created_at, updated_at, runs_count
                FROM processes ORDER BY updated_at DESC
            """).fetchall()
            return [dict(row) for row in rows]
    
    def update(self, process_id: str, name: str = None, description: str = None, 
               nodes: List = None, edges: List = None):
        updates = []
        params = []
        
        if name:
            updates.append("name = ?")
            params.append(name)
        if description is not None:
            updates.append("description = ?")
            params.append(description)
        if nodes:
            updates.append("nodes = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(nodes))
        if edges:
            updates.append("edges = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(edges))
        
        if updates:
            updates.append("updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                conn.execute(f"UPDATE processes SET {', '.join(updates)} WHERE id = ?", params + [process_id])
    
    def delete(self, process_id: str):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
    
    def record_run(self, process_id: str, status: str, output: str = "", error: str = ""):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO process_runs (process_id, started_at, completed_at, status, output, error)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, datetime.now().isoformat(), datetime.now().isoformat(), status, output, error))
            
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE processes SET status = ?, last_run = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, runs_count = runs_count + 1
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (status, process_id))
    
    async def _execute_by_ref(self, executor_ref: str, node_config: Dict, context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Выполнение узла через executor_ref"""
        from core.skill_executor import SkillExecutor
        
        parts = executor_ref.split(':', 1)
        if len(parts) != 2:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Invalid executor_ref format: {executor_ref}"}
        
        ref_type, ref_id = parts
        
        if ref_type == 'builtin':
            # Встроенные экзекьюторы
            if ref_id == 'start_node':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Process started"}}
            elif ref_id == 'end_node':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Process completed"}}
            elif ref_id == 'condition_evaluator':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"condition": True}}
            elif ref_id == 'agent_generic':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Generic agent executed"}}
            elif ref_id == 'http_executor':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "HTTP executor (placeholder)"}}
            elif ref_id == 'script_executor':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Script executor (placeholder)"}}
            else:
                return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown builtin executor: {ref_id}"}
        
        elif ref_type == 'skill':
            # Выполняем скилл через SkillExecutor
            try:
                executor = SkillExecutor(db_path=self.db_path)
                # Получаем skill из БД
                conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
                conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("SELECT data, name FROM components WHERE id = ? AND type = 'skill'", (ref_id,))
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                conn.close()
                
                if not row:
                    # Создаем HITL задачу
                    return await self._create_hitl_task(node_config, context, f"Skill {ref_id} not found")
                
                skill_data = row['data']
                if isinstance(skill_data, str):
                    skill_data = json.loads(skill_data)
                
                # Подготавливаем входные параметры
                input_params = node_config.get('data', {})
                input_params['context'] = context
                
                # Выполняем
                result = executor.execute(skill_data, input_params)
                
                return {
                    "success": result.status == "success",
                    "result": result.output if hasattr(result, 'output') else result,
                    "error": result.error if hasattr(result, 'error') else None
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return await self._create_hitl_task(node_config, context, f"Skill execution error: {str(e)}")
        
        else:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown executor type: {ref_type}"}
    
    async def _create_hitl_task(self, node_config: Dict, context: Dict, reason: str) -> Dict:
        """Создает HITL задачу, когда компонент не найден"""
        node_id = node_config.get('id', 'unknown')
        node_label = node_config.get('data', {}).get('label', 'Unknown task')
        
        task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            # Проверяем, существует ли таблица tasks
            cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='tasks'")
            if cursor.fetchone():
                cursor.execute('''
                    INSERT INTO tasks (id, title, description, status, created_at, context)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ''', (
                    task_id,
                    f"HITL: {node_label}",
                    f"Manual execution required: {reason}",
                    "pending",
                    datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    json.dumps({"node_config": node_config, "context": context})
                ))
                conn.commit()
            
            conn.close()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Failed to create HITL task: {e}")
        
        return {
            "success": False,
            "error": f"HITL task created: {reason}",
            "hitl_task_id": task_id,
            "requires_approval": True
        }
    
    async def execute_node(self, node_id: str, node_config: Dict, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет один узел процесса с поддержкой executor_ref"""
        from nodes import NodeRegistry
        
        print(f"🔍 process_manager: node_config = {node_config}")
        node_type = node_config.get('data', {}).get('node_type', node_config.get('type'))
        
        # Проверяем наличие executor_ref
        executor_ref = node_config.get('data', {}).get('executor_ref')
        
        if executor_ref:
            # Используем executor_ref для выполнения
            print(f"🎯 Using executor_ref: {executor_ref} for node {node_id}")
            result = await self._execute_by_ref(executor_ref, node_config, context)
            return result
        
        # Fallback на старую логику через NodeRegistry
        if not node_type:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Node type not specified for {node_id}"}
        
        node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
        if not node_instance:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"}
        
        # Извлекаем конфигурацию узла
        config = node_config.get('data', {})
        # Убираем мета-поля
        for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color', 'executor_ref']:
            config.pop(key, None)
        
        # Добавляем ID узла в контекст
        if context is None:
            context = {}
        context['current_node_id'] = node_id
        
        print(f"🔧 Выполнение узла {node_id} (тип: {node_type})")
        
        try:
            result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
            return {
                "success": result.get("success", True),
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "result": result
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "error": str(e)
            }
    
    async def execute_process(self, process_id: str, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет весь процесс с поддержкой condition узлов"""
        print(f"🔍 [execute_process] process_id={process_id}, context={context}")
        process = self.get(process_id)
        if not process:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Process not found"}
        
        nodes = process.get('nodes', [])
        edges = process.get('edges', [])
        
        # Строим граф зависимостей
        node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}
        children_map = {}
        for edge in edges:
            source = edge.get('source')
            target = edge.get('target')
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
        
        print(f"📊 Children map: {children_map}")
        
        # Находим стартовые узлы (без входящих рёбер)
        all_targets = set(edge.get('target') for edge in edges)
        start_nodes = [node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] not in all_targets]
        
        print(f"📍 Start nodes: {start_nodes}")
        
        if not start_nodes and nodes:
            start_nodes = [nodes[0]['id']]
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'running')
        
        results = {}
        queue = list(start_nodes)
        visited = set()
        
        while queue:
            node_id = queue.pop(0)
            print(f"🔄 Processing node: {node_id}")
            
            if node_id in visited:
                print(f"  ⏭️ Already visited: {node_id}")
                continue
            visited.add(node_id)
            
            node = node_map.get(node_id)
            if not node:
                print(f"  ❌ Node not found: {node_id}")
                continue
            
            result = await self.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
            results[node_id] = result
            print(f"  📊 Result: success={result.get('success')}")
            
            if result.get('success', False):
                # Получаем следующий узел
                next_nodes = children_map.get(node_id, [])
                print(f"  ➡️ Next nodes from map: {next_nodes}")
                
                # Если текущий узел - condition, используем его next_node
                node_type = node.get('data', {}).get('node_type')
                if node_type == 'condition' and result.get('result', {}).get('next_node'):
                    next_nodes = [result['result']['next_node']]
                    print(f"  🔀 Condition branch: {next_nodes}")
                
                for next_id in next_nodes:
                    if next_id not in visited:
                        print(f"  📌 Adding to queue: {next_id}")
                        queue.append(next_id)
            else:
                print(f"  ❌ Node failed, stopping execution")
                self.record_run(process_id, 'failed')
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "process_id": process_id,
                    "results": results,
                    "error": f"Node {node_id} failed"
                }
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'completed')
        return {
            "success": True,
            "process_id": process_id,
            "results": results
        }

    def substitute_vars(self, node: Dict, variables: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Подстановка переменных в конфигурацию узла"""
        import re
        
        def replace_var(value):
            if isinstance(value, str):
                def replacer(match):
                    var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
                    return str(variables.get(var_name, match.group(0)))
                return re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replacer, value)
            elif isinstance(value, dict):
                return {k: replace_var(v) for k, v in value.items()}
            elif isinstance(value, list):
                return [replace_var(item) for item in value]
            return value
        
        if "data" in node:
            node["data"] = replace_var(node["data"])
        return node

# Синглтон
_manager = None

def get_manager():
    global _manager
    if _manager is None:
        _manager = ProcessManager()
    return _manager
```

```bash
cd ~/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta && python3 << 'EOF'
import asyncio
import json
import sqlite3

async def test():
    print("=" * 50)
    print("Тестирование executor_ref (с await)")
    print("=" * 50)
    
    db_path = "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/data/kymatics.db"
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Получаем скилл
    cursor.execute("SELECT id, name, data FROM components WHERE type='skill' LIMIT 1")
    skill = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not skill:
        print("❌ Нет скиллов в БД")
        return
    
    skill_id, skill_name, skill_data_raw = skill
    print(f"\n1. Скилл: {skill_name} (id: {skill_id[:8]}...)")
    
    # Парсим data
    if skill_data_raw:
        try:
            skill_data = json.loads(skill_data_raw)
            print(f"   data type: {type(skill_data)}")
            if isinstance(skill_data, dict):
                print(f"   keys: {list(skill_data.keys())}")
        except:
            skill_data = {"content": str(skill_data_raw)}
    else:
        skill_data = {"content": "print('Hello from skill')"}
    
    # Тестируем SkillExecutor
    from core.skill_executor import SkillExecutor
    executor = SkillExecutor(db_path=db_path)
    
    input_params = {"message": "test"}
    print(f"\n2. Выполняем скилл с параметрами: {input_params}")
    
    try:
        result = await executor.execute_skill(skill_data, input_params)
        print(f"\n3. Результат:")
        print(f"   status: {result.status}")
        print(f"   output: {result.output}")
        print(f"   error: {result.error}")
        
        if result.status == "success":
            print("\n✅ Скилл выполнен успешно!")
        else:
            print(f"\n⚠️ Ошибка: {result.error}")
    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        print(f"\n❌ Исключение: {e}")
        traceback.print_exc()
    
    conn.close()

asyncio.run(test())
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Хорошо. Вот полная рабочая версия `process_manager.py` с методом `_init_db`:

```bash
cat > ~/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/process_manager.py << 'EOF'
"""
Process Manager — управление процессами через React Flow
"""
import json
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from contextlib import contextmanager
import os

# Dynamic path - use environment variable or default to local processes.db
DB_PATH = Path(os.getenv("PROCESSES_DB_PATH", str(Path(__file__).parent / "processes.db")))

class ProcessManager:
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = None):
        self.db_path = db_path or str(DB_PATH)
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """Initialize database tables if they don't exist"""
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            # Создаем таблицу processes если нет
            conn.execute('''
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processes (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT NOT NULL,
                    description TEXT,
                    nodes TEXT,
                    edges TEXT,
                    status TEXT DEFAULT 'draft',
                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    last_run TIMESTAMP,
                    runs_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0
                )
            ''')
            # Создаем таблицу process_runs если нет
            conn.execute('''
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS process_runs (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    process_id TEXT,
                    started_at TIMESTAMP,
                    completed_at TIMESTAMP,
                    status TEXT,
                    output TEXT,
                    error TEXT,
                    FOREIGN KEY (process_id) REFERENCES processes(id)
                )
            ''')
            # Создаем таблицу components если нет
            conn.execute('''
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS components (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    name TEXT NOT NULL,
                    description TEXT,
                    tags TEXT,
                    data TEXT,
                    status TEXT DEFAULT 'draft',
                    version TEXT DEFAULT '1.0.0',
                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
            ''')
            # Создаем таблицу tasks если нет (для HITL)
            conn.execute('''
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    title TEXT NOT NULL,
                    description TEXT,
                    status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    context TEXT,
                    assigned_to TEXT,
                    resolved_at TIMESTAMP
                )
            ''')
    
    @contextmanager
    def _get_connection(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        try:
            yield conn
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    def create(self, name: str, nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict], description: str = "") -> str:
        """Создаёт новый процесс"""
        process_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO processes (id, name, description, nodes, edges, status)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, name, description, json.dumps(nodes), json.dumps(edges), 'stopped'))
        
        return process_id
    
    def get(self, process_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,)).fetchone()
            if row:
                return {
                    'id': row['id'],
                    'name': row['name'],
                    'description': row['description'],
                    'nodes': json.loads(row['nodes']),
                    'edges': json.loads(row['edges']),
                    'status': row['status'],
                    'created_at': row['created_at'],
                    'updated_at': row['updated_at'],
                    'last_run': row['last_run'],
                    'runs_count': row['runs_count']
                }
        return None
    
    def list_all(self) -> List[Dict]:
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, description, status, created_at, updated_at, runs_count
                FROM processes ORDER BY updated_at DESC
            """).fetchall()
            return [dict(row) for row in rows]
    
    def update(self, process_id: str, name: str = None, description: str = None, 
               nodes: List = None, edges: List = None):
        updates = []
        params = []
        
        if name:
            updates.append("name = ?")
            params.append(name)
        if description is not None:
            updates.append("description = ?")
            params.append(description)
        if nodes:
            updates.append("nodes = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(nodes))
        if edges:
            updates.append("edges = ?")
            params.append(json.dumps(edges))
        
        if updates:
            updates.append("updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP")
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                conn.execute(f"UPDATE processes SET {', '.join(updates)} WHERE id = ?", params + [process_id])
    
    def delete(self, process_id: str):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM processes WHERE id = ?", (process_id,))
    
    def record_run(self, process_id: str, status: str, output: str = "", error: str = ""):
        with self._get_connection() as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO process_runs (process_id, started_at, completed_at, status, output, error)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (process_id, datetime.now().isoformat(), datetime.now().isoformat(), status, output, error))
            
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE processes SET status = ?, last_run = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, runs_count = runs_count + 1
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (status, process_id))
    
    async def _execute_by_ref(self, executor_ref: str, node_config: Dict, context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Выполнение узла через executor_ref"""
        from core.skill_executor import SkillExecutor
        
        parts = executor_ref.split(':', 1)
        if len(parts) != 2:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Invalid executor_ref format: {executor_ref}"}
        
        ref_type, ref_id = parts
        
        if ref_type == 'builtin':
            # Встроенные экзекьюторы
            if ref_id == 'start_node':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Process started"}}
            elif ref_id == 'end_node':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Process completed"}}
            elif ref_id == 'condition_evaluator':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"condition": True}}
            elif ref_id == 'agent_generic':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Generic agent executed"}}
            elif ref_id == 'http_executor':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "HTTP executor (placeholder)"}}
            elif ref_id == 'script_executor':
                return {"success": True, "result": {"message": "Script executor (placeholder)"}}
            else:
                return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown builtin executor: {ref_id}"}
        
        elif ref_type == 'skill':
            # Выполняем скилл по ID
            try:
                executor = SkillExecutor(db_path=self.db_path)
                # Подготавливаем входные параметры
                input_params = node_config.get('data', {})
                input_params['context'] = context
                
                # Выполняем скилл по ID
                result_dict = await executor.execute_skill(ref_id, input_params, context)
                
                return {
                    "success": result_dict.get('status') == 'success',
                    "result": result_dict.get('output', result_dict.get('result')),
                    "error": result_dict.get('error')
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return await self._create_hitl_task(node_config, context, f"Skill execution error: {str(e)}")
        
        else:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown executor type: {ref_type}"}
    
    async def _create_hitl_task(self, node_config: Dict, context: Dict, reason: str) -> Dict:
        """Создает HITL задачу, когда компонент не найден"""
        node_id = node_config.get('id', 'unknown')
        node_label = node_config.get('data', {}).get('label', 'Unknown task')
        
        task_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute('''
                INSERT INTO tasks (id, title, description, status, created_at, context)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            ''', (
                task_id,
                f"HITL: {node_label}",
                f"Manual execution required: {reason}",
                "pending",
                datetime.now().isoformat(),
                json.dumps({"node_config": node_config, "context": context})
            ))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Failed to create HITL task: {e}")
        
        return {
            "success": False,
            "error": f"HITL task created: {reason}",
            "hitl_task_id": task_id,
            "requires_approval": True
        }
    
    async def execute_node(self, node_id: str, node_config: Dict, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет один узел процесса с поддержкой executor_ref"""
        from nodes import NodeRegistry
        
        print(f"🔍 process_manager: node_config = {node_config}")
        node_type = node_config.get('data', {}).get('node_type', node_config.get('type'))
        
        # Проверяем наличие executor_ref
        executor_ref = node_config.get('data', {}).get('executor_ref')
        
        if executor_ref:
            # Используем executor_ref для выполнения
            print(f"🎯 Using executor_ref: {executor_ref} for node {node_id}")
            result = await self._execute_by_ref(executor_ref, node_config, context)
            return result
        
        # Fallback на старую логику через NodeRegistry
        if not node_type:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Node type not specified for {node_id}"}
        
        node_instance = NodeRegistry.get_instance(node_type)
        if not node_instance:
            return {"success": False, "error": f"Unknown node type: {node_type}"}
        
        # Извлекаем конфигурацию узла
        config = node_config.get('data', {})
        # Убираем мета-поля
        for key in ['label', 'node_type', 'bg_color', 'executor_ref']:
            config.pop(key, None)
        
        # Добавляем ID узла в контекст
        if context is None:
            context = {}
        context['current_node_id'] = node_id
        
        print(f"🔧 Выполнение узла {node_id} (тип: {node_type})")
        
        try:
            result = await node_instance.execute(context, config)
            return {
                "success": result.get("success", True),
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "result": result
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "node_id": node_id,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "error": str(e)
            }
    
    async def execute_process(self, process_id: str, context: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Выполняет весь процесс с поддержкой condition узлов"""
        print(f"🔍 [execute_process] process_id={process_id}, context={context}")
        process = self.get(process_id)
        if not process:
            return {"success": False, "error": "Process not found"}
        
        nodes = process.get('nodes', [])
        edges = process.get('edges', [])
        
        # Строим граф зависимостей
        node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}
        children_map = {}
        for edge in edges:
            source = edge.get('source')
            target = edge.get('target')
            if source not in children_map:
                children_map[source] = []
            children_map[source].append(target)
        
        print(f"📊 Children map: {children_map}")
        
        # Находим стартовые узлы (без входящих рёбер)
        all_targets = set(edge.get('target') for edge in edges)
        start_nodes = [node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] not in all_targets]
        
        print(f"📍 Start nodes: {start_nodes}")
        
        if not start_nodes and nodes:
            start_nodes = [nodes[0]['id']]
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'running')
        
        results = {}
        queue = list(start_nodes)
        visited = set()
        
        while queue:
            node_id = queue.pop(0)
            print(f"🔄 Processing node: {node_id}")
            
            if node_id in visited:
                print(f"  ⏭️ Already visited: {node_id}")
                continue
            visited.add(node_id)
            
            node = node_map.get(node_id)
            if not node:
                print(f"  ❌ Node not found: {node_id}")
                continue
            
            result = await self.execute_node(node_id, node, context)
            results[node_id] = result
            print(f"  📊 Result: success={result.get('success')}")
            
            if result.get('success', False):
                # Получаем следующий узел
                next_nodes = children_map.get(node_id, [])
                print(f"  ➡️ Next nodes from map: {next_nodes}")
                
                # Если текущий узел - condition, используем его next_node
                node_type = node.get('data', {}).get('node_type')
                if node_type == 'condition' and result.get('result', {}).get('next_node'):
                    next_nodes = [result['result']['next_node']]
                    print(f"  🔀 Condition branch: {next_nodes}")
                
                for next_id in next_nodes:
                    if next_id not in visited:
                        print(f"  📌 Adding to queue: {next_id}")
                        queue.append(next_id)
            else:
                print(f"  ❌ Node failed, stopping execution")
                self.record_run(process_id, 'failed')
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "process_id": process_id,
                    "results": results,
                    "error": f"Node {node_id} failed"
                }
        
        self.record_run(process_id, 'completed')
        return {
            "success": True,
            "process_id": process_id,
            "results": results
        }

    def substitute_vars(self, node: Dict, variables: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Подстановка переменных в конфигурацию узла"""
        import re
        
        def replace_var(value):
            if isinstance(value, str):
                def replacer(match):
                    var_name = match.group(1) or match.group(2)
                    return str(variables.get(var_name, match.group(0)))
                return re.sub(r'\$\{([^}]+)\}|\$([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', replacer, value)
            elif isinstance(value, dict):
                return {k: replace_var(v) for k, v in value.items()}
            elif isinstance(value, list):
                return [replace_var(item) for item in value]
            return value
        
        if "data" in node:
            node["data"] = replace_var(node["data"])
        return node

# Синглтон
_manager = None

def get_manager():
    global _manager
    if _manager is None:
        _manager = ProcessManager()
    return _manager
EOF
```

### ✅ Итоги выполнения 92.1 (Исполняемые узлы процессов)

"""Command safety analyzer for hybrid command execution"""

from typing import List, Tuple
from enum import Enum

class SafetyLevel(Enum):
    SAFE = "safe"
    WARNING = "warning"
    DANGEROUS = "dangerous"

# Known dangerous commands
DANGEROUS_COMMANDS = ['sudo', 'dd', 'mkfs', 'poweroff', 'reboot', 'shutdown']

# Commands that require approval
WARNING_COMMANDS = ['rm', 'mv', 'chmod', 'chown', 'kill', 'killall', 'pkill']

def analyze_command(command: str) -> Tuple[SafetyLevel, List[str]]:
    command_lower = command.lower().strip()
    first_word = command_lower.split()[0] if command_lower.split() else ""
    matches = []
    
    # Check for dangerous commands
    for cmd in DANGEROUS_COMMANDS:
        if first_word == cmd or cmd in command_lower.split():
            matches.append(f"DANGEROUS: {cmd}")
            return SafetyLevel.DANGEROUS, matches
    
    # Check for warning commands
    for cmd in WARNING_COMMANDS:
        if first_word == cmd or cmd in command_lower.split():
            matches.append(f"WARNING: {cmd}")
            return SafetyLevel.WARNING, matches
    
    return SafetyLevel.SAFE, []

def is_safe_command(command: str) -> bool:
    level, _ = analyze_command(command)
    return level == SafetyLevel.SAFE

def requires_approval(command: str) -> bool:
    level, _ = analyze_command(command)
    return level in [SafetyLevel.WARNING, SafetyLevel.DANGEROUS]

(.venv) kirillkravcov@Mac batuta % cat ~/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_safe_execute.py
import subprocess
import os
import json
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from command_safety import analyze_command, SafetyLevel

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/execute", tags=["execute"])

class CommandRequest(BaseModel):
    command: str
    cwd: Optional[str] = None
    approved: bool = False

class CommandResponse(BaseModel):
    stdout: str
    stderr: str
    exit_code: int
    safety_level: str
    requires_approval: bool
    matches: List[str]

class ApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    command: str
    cwd: Optional[str] = None
    execution_id: str

# Store pending approvals (in production use Redis/DB)
pending_approvals = {}

@router.post("/safe", response_model=CommandResponse)
async def execute_safe_command(req: CommandRequest):
    """Execute command with safety checks"""
    
    # Analyze command
    safety_level, matches = analyze_command(req.command)
    requires_approval = safety_level != SafetyLevel.SAFE
    
    # For dangerous commands, require explicit approval
    if safety_level == SafetyLevel.DANGEROUS and not req.approved:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail=f"DANGEROUS command requires approval. Matches: {matches}"
        )
    
    # For warning commands, can proceed if approved
    if safety_level == SafetyLevel.WARNING and not req.approved:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403,
            detail=f"Command requires approval. Matches: {matches}"
        )
    
    # Execute command
    try:
        cwd = req.cwd or os.getcwd()
        result = subprocess.run(
            req.command,
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            cwd=cwd,
            timeout=60
        )
        
        return CommandResponse(
            stdout=result.stdout.strip(),
            stderr=result.stderr.strip(),
            exit_code=result.returncode,
            safety_level=safety_level.value,
            requires_approval=False,
            matches=matches
        )
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=408, detail="Command timeout (60s)")
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.post("/request-approval")
async def request_approval(req: CommandRequest):
    """Request approval for a command (creates HITL task)"""
    
    safety_level, matches = analyze_command(req.command)
    
    # Create approval request
    execution_id = f"cmd_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}_{id(req)}"
    
    pending_approvals[execution_id] = {
        "command": req.command,
        "cwd": req.cwd,
        "safety_level": safety_level.value,
        "matches": matches,
        "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "status": "pending"
    }
    
    # TODO: Create HITL task in the system
    # This will be integrated with Tasks API
    
    return {
        "execution_id": execution_id,
        "command": req.command,
        "safety_level": safety_level.value,
        "matches": matches,
        "message": "Command requires approval. Use /approve <id> or /reject <id>"
    }

@router.post("/approve/{execution_id}")
async def approve_command(execution_id: str):
    """Approve a pending command"""
    if execution_id not in pending_approvals:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Approval request not found")
    
    approval = pending_approvals[execution_id]
    approval["status"] = "approved"
    
    # Execute the approved command
    try:
        cwd = approval.get("cwd") or os.getcwd()
        result = subprocess.run(
            approval["command"],
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            cwd=cwd,
            timeout=60
        )
        
        return {
            "execution_id": execution_id,
            "status": "executed",
            "stdout": result.stdout.strip(),
            "stderr": result.stderr.strip(),
            "exit_code": result.returncode
        }
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@router.post("/reject/{execution_id}")
async def reject_command(execution_id: str):
    """Reject a pending command"""
    if execution_id not in pending_approvals:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Approval request not found")
    
    pending_approvals[execution_id]["status"] = "rejected"
    return {"execution_id": execution_id, "status": "rejected", "message": "Command rejected"}

**Действие 8: Создаем компоненты для терминала с поддержкой безопасности**

Сначала создадим хук для работы с безопасными командами:

```bash
mkdir -p ~/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/react-editor/src/hooks

cat > ~/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/react-editor/src/hooks/useSafeCommand.ts << 'EOF'
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';

const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute';

export interface CommandAnalysis {
  command: string;
  safety_level: 'safe' | 'warning' | 'dangerous';
  requires_approval: boolean;
  matches: string[];
  is_dangerous: boolean;
  is_warning: boolean;
  is_safe: boolean;
}

export interface ExecutionResult {
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
  exit_code: number;
  safety_level: string;
  requires_approval: boolean;
  matches: string[];
}

export interface PendingApproval {
  execution_id: string;
  command: string;
  safety_level: string;
  matches: string[];
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'executed';
}

export function useSafeCommand() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [analysis, setAnalysis] = useState<CommandAnalysis | null>(null);
  const [pendingApprovals, setPendingApprovals] = useState<PendingApproval[]>([]);

  // Анализ команды
  const analyzeCommand = useCallback(async (command: string): Promise<CommandAnalysis | null> => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/analyze`, { command });
      setAnalysis(response.data);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to analyze command');
      return null;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Выполнение команды (с подтверждением)
  const executeCommand = useCallback(async (
    command: string, 
    cwd?: string, 
    approved: boolean = false
  ): Promise<ExecutionResult | null> => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/safe`, { command, cwd, approved });
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      if (err.response?.status === 403) {
        setError(err.response.data.detail);
      } else {
        setError(err.message || 'Failed to execute command');
      }
      return null;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Запрос подтверждения (создает HITL задачу)
  const requestApproval = useCallback(async (command: string, cwd?: string): Promise<PendingApproval | null> => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/request-approval`, { command, cwd });
      const approval: PendingApproval = {
        execution_id: response.data.execution_id,
        command: response.data.command,
        safety_level: response.data.safety_level,
        matches: response.data.matches,
        status: 'pending'
      };
      setPendingApprovals(prev => [...prev, approval]);
      return approval;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to request approval');
      return null;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  // Подтверждение команды
  const approveCommand = useCallback(async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/approve/${execution_id}`);
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'executed' } : p
      ));
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to approve command');
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Отклонение команды
  const rejectCommand = useCallback(async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/reject/${execution_id}`);
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'rejected' } : p
      ));
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to reject command');
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    loading,
    error,
    analysis,
    pendingApprovals,
    analyzeCommand,
    executeCommand,
    requestApproval,
    approveCommand,
    rejectCommand,
    clearError: () => setError(null),
  };
}
EOF

echo "✅ useSafeCommand.ts создан"
```

**Действие 9: Создаем компонент модального окна подтверждения**

```bash
mkdir -p ~/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/react-editor/src/components/Terminal

cat > ~/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/react-editor/src/components/Terminal/ConfirmationModal.tsx << 'EOF'
import React from 'react';

interface ConfirmationModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  command: string;
  safetyLevel: 'warning' | 'dangerous';
  matches: string[];
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
  loading?: boolean;
}

export const ConfirmationModal: React.FC<ConfirmationModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  command,
  safetyLevel,
  matches,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  loading = false,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isDangerous = safetyLevel === 'dangerous';
  const bgColor = isDangerous ? 'bg-red-900' : 'bg-yellow-800';
  const borderColor = isDangerous ? 'border-red-500' : 'border-yellow-500';
  const titleColor = isDangerous ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-yellow-400';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black bg-opacity-70">
      <div className={`${bgColor} border ${borderColor} rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full mx-4`}>
        <div className="p-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <span className={`text-2xl ${titleColor}`}>
              {isDangerous ? '⚠️⚠️⚠️' : '⚠️'}
            </span>
            <h2 className={`text-xl font-bold ${titleColor}`}>
              {isDangerous ? 'ОПАСНАЯ КОМАНДА!' : 'Требуется подтверждение'}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <p className="text-gray-300 mb-2">Команда:</p>
            <code className="block bg-black bg-opacity-50 p-3 rounded text-sm font-mono text-green-400 overflow-x-auto">
              {command}
            </code>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <p className="text-gray-300 mb-2">Причина:</p>
            <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-yellow-400">
              {matches.map((match, idx) => (
                <li key={idx}>{match}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>

          {isDangerous && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-900 bg-opacity-50 rounded border border-red-700">
              <p className="text-red-300 text-sm">
                ⚠️ Эта команда может нанести непоправимый вред системе! 
                Вы уверены, что хотите её выполнить?
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 mt-6">
            <button
              onClick={onCancel}
              disabled={loading}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded text-white transition disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Отмена
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={onConfirm}
              disabled={loading}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded text-white transition disabled:opacity-50 ${
                isDangerous 
                  ? 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700' 
                  : 'bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700'
              }`}
            >
              {loading ? 'Выполнение...' : 'Подтвердить и выполнить'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
EOF
```

**Действие 10: Создаем основной компонент терминала**

```bash
cat > ~/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/react-editor/src/components/Terminal/SafeTerminal.tsx << 'EOF'
import React, { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useSafeCommand } from '../../hooks/useSafeCommand';
import { ConfirmationModal } from './ConfirmationModal';

interface TerminalMessage {
  id: string;
  type: 'input' | 'output' | 'error' | 'info';
  content: string;
  timestamp: Date;
}

interface SafeTerminalProps {
  cwd?: string;
  onCommandExecuted?: (command: string, result: any) => void;
}

export const SafeTerminal: React.FC<SafeTerminalProps> = ({ 
  cwd, 
  onCommandExecuted 
}) => {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<TerminalMessage[]>([
    { 
      id: 'welcome', 
      type: 'info', 
      content: 'Welcome to Safe Terminal. Type /help for commands.', 
      timestamp: new Date() 
    }
  ]);
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [pendingCommand, setPendingCommand] = useState<{
    command: string;
    analysis: any;
  } | null>(null);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  
  const { 
    loading, 
    error, 
    analyzeCommand, 
    executeCommand, 
    requestApproval,
    approveCommand,
    rejectCommand,
    pendingApprovals,
    clearError 
  } = useSafeCommand();

  const scrollToBottom = () => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    scrollToBottom();
  }, [messages]);

  const addMessage = (type: TerminalMessage['type'], content: string) => {
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      type,
      content,
      timestamp: new Date()
    }]);
  };

  const handleExecuteCommand = useCallback(async (command: string, approved: boolean = false) => {
    addMessage('input', `$ ${command}`);
    
    // Обработка специальных команд
    if (command === '/help') {
      addMessage('info', `Доступные команды:
  /help        - показать эту справку
  /clear       - очистить терминал
  /approve <id> - подтвердить выполнение команды
  /reject <id>  - отклонить выполнение команды
  /pending     - показать ожидающие подтверждения`);
      return;
    }
    
    if (command === '/clear') {
      setMessages([]);
      return;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/approve ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await approveCommand(id);
        addMessage('info', `✅ Команда ${id} подтверждена`);
      } else {
        addMessage('error', '❌ Укажите ID команды: /approve <id>');
      }
      return;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/reject ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await rejectCommand(id);
        addMessage('info', `❌ Команда ${id} отклонена`);
      } else {
        addMessage('error', '❌ Укажите ID команды: /reject <id>');
      }
      return;
    }
    
    if (command === '/pending') {
      if (pendingApprovals.length === 0) {
        addMessage('info', 'Нет ожидающих подтверждения команд');
      } else {
        addMessage('info', 'Ожидающие подтверждения команды:');
        pendingApprovals.forEach(p => {
          addMessage('info', `  ${p.execution_id}: ${p.command} (${p.safety_level})`);
        });
      }
      return;
    }
    
    // Анализ команды
    const analysis = await analyzeCommand(command);
    if (!analysis) {
      addMessage('error', `Ошибка анализа команды: ${error}`);
      return;
    }
    
    // Определяем действие по уровню безопасности
    if (analysis.is_safe) {
      // SAFE - выполняем сразу
      const result = await executeCommand(command, cwd, false);
      if (result) {
        if (result.stdout) addMessage('output', result.stdout);
        if (result.stderr) addMessage('error', result.stderr);
        onCommandExecuted?.(command, result);
      } else if (error) {
        addMessage('error', error);
      }
    } 
    else if (analysis.is_warning) {
      // WARNING - показываем модальное окно
      setPendingCommand({ command, analysis });
      setIsModalOpen(true);
    } 
    else if (analysis.is_dangerous) {
      // DANGEROUS - создаем HITL задачу
      addMessage('info', `⚠️ Опасная команда! Создаю задачу для подтверждения...`);
      const approval = await requestApproval(command, cwd);
      if (approval) {
        addMessage('info', `📋 Создана задача подтверждения: ${approval.execution_id}`);
        addMessage('info', `   Используйте /approve ${approval.execution_id} для выполнения или /reject ${approval.execution_id} для отклонения`);
      } else {
        addMessage('error', `Не удалось создать задачу подтверждения: ${error}`);
      }
    }
  }, [analyzeCommand, executeCommand, requestApproval, approveCommand, rejectCommand, pendingApprovals, cwd, error, onCommandExecuted]);

  const handleConfirmModal = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!pendingCommand) return;
    
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    const { command, analysis } = pendingCommand;
    
    addMessage('info', `⚠️ Команда требует подтверждения: ${analysis.matches.join(', ')}`);
    
    const result = await executeCommand(command, cwd, true);
    if (result) {
      if (result.stdout) addMessage('output', result.stdout);
      if (result.stderr) addMessage('error', result.stderr);
      onCommandExecuted?.(command, result);
    } else if (error) {
      addMessage('error', error);
    }
    
    setPendingCommand(null);
  }, [pendingCommand, executeCommand, cwd, error, onCommandExecuted]);

  const handleCancelModal = useCallback(() => {
    setIsModalOpen(false);
    setPendingCommand(null);
    addMessage('info', '❌ Команда отклонена');
  }, []);

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && inputValue.trim() && !loading) {
      handleExecuteCommand(inputValue.trim());
      setInputValue('');
    }
  };

  const getMessageStyle = (type: TerminalMessage['type']) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'input': return 'text-green-400';
      case 'output': return 'text-gray-300';
      case 'error': return 'text-red-400';
      case 'info': return 'text-blue-400';
      default: return 'text-gray-300';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-black text-white font-mono">
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-1">
        {messages.map((msg) => (
          <div key={msg.id} className={getMessageStyle(msg.type)}>
            {msg.type === 'input' && <span className="text-gray-500">$ </span>}
            <span className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">{msg.content}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
        {loading && (
          <div className="text-yellow-400 animate-pulse">
            Выполнение...
          </div>
        )}
        <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
      </div>
      
      <div className="border-t border-gray-800 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-green-400">$</span>
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={inputValue}
            onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
            onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent outline-none text-white font-mono disabled:opacity-50"
            placeholder="Введите команду..."
            autoFocus
          />
        </div>
        {error && (
          <div className="text-red-400 text-sm mt-2">
            ❌ {error}
            <button 
              onClick={clearError} 
              className="ml-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white"
            >
              [x]
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      
      <ConfirmationModal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        command={pendingCommand?.command || ''}
        safetyLevel={pendingCommand?.analysis?.safety_level || 'warning'}
        matches={pendingCommand?.analysis?.matches || []}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmModal}
        onCancel={handleCancelModal}
        loading={loading}
      />
    </div>
  );
};
EOF

echo "✅ SafeTerminal.tsx создан"
```
