(.venv) kirillkravcov@Mac UniversalCreativeHub % cat /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/pages/TerminalPage.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useChatStore } from '../features/chat/store/chatStore';
import { getTaskStatus, getTasks } from '../batuta';
import { ChatHistory } from '../features/chat/components/ChatHistory';
import { UnifiedRightPanelIDE } from '../features/ide/UnifiedRightPanelIDE';
import { useChatDirect } from '../features/chat/hooks/useChatDirect';
import { decomposeTask } from '../batuta/decompose';
import { WorkspaceIDE, useProjects } from '../features/ide';
import { useTerminalCommand } from '../features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand';

type TerminalMode = 'chat' | 'terminal';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed';
}

interface Process {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'generated' | 'executing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  nodes?: any[];
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Component {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  version: string;
}

export const TerminalPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { chats, activeChatId, addChat, addMessage, updateMessageStatus } = useChatStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TerminalMode>('chat');
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [tasksTab, setTasksTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [processTab, setProcessTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [components, setComponents] = useState<Component[]>([]);
  const [isCreatingProcess, setIsCreatingProcess] = useState(false);
  const [isWaitingResponse, setIsWaitingResponse] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const { sendMessage, isLoading, error: chatError, messages: directMessages } = useChatDirect();
  const { selectedProjectId, selectProject } = useProjects();
  const { executeCommand, output, isExecuting, clearOutput } = useTerminalCommand();
  const processedMessageIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  const currentChat = chats.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);
  const messages = currentChat?.messages || [];

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const loadTasks = async () => {
    try {
      const batutaTasks = await getTasks();
      const mappedTasks: Task[] = batutaTasks
        .filter(t => t.status !== 'archived')
        .map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          description: t.description,
          status: t.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : (t.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'pending')
        }));
      setTasks(mappedTasks);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load tasks:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/process/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setProcesses(data.processes || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadComponents = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/components/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setComponents(data.components || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load components:', error);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
    loadProcesses();
    loadComponents();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadTasks();
      loadProcesses();
      loadComponents();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChatId) return;
    
    directMessages.forEach(msg => {
      if (!processedMessageIds.current.has(msg.id)) {
        processedMessageIds.current.add(msg.id);
        
        if (msg.role === 'user') {
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'user',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        } else if (msg.role === 'agent') {
          setIsWaitingResponse(false);
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }, [directMessages, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chatError && activeChatId) {
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      const errorMessageId = `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
      addMessage(activeChatId, {
        id: errorMessageId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${chatError}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  }, [chatError, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  const createProcessFromDecomposition = async (taskText: string) => {
    if (!activeChatId || isCreatingProcess) return;
    
    setIsCreatingProcess(true);
    const processingMsgId = `process_gen_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
    addMessage(activeChatId, {
      id: processingMsgId,
      role: 'assistant',
      content: 'Creating process from decomposition...',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'in_progress',
    });
    
    try {
      const decomposition = await decomposeTask(
        taskText.substring(0, 100),
        taskText
      );
      
      const subtasksText = decomposition.subtasks
        .map((st, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${st.title}: ${st.description}`)
        .join('\n');
      
      const generationPrompt = `Create BPMN process based on these steps:\n\n${subtasksText}\n\nProcess name: ${decomposition.title}`;

      const generateResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/design', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          requirement: generationPrompt,
          project_id: null 
        }),
      });
      
      if (!generateResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
      }
      
      const generated = await generateResponse.json();
      
      const saveResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: decomposition.title,
          description: `Generated from: ${taskText.substring(0, 200)}`,
          nodes: generated.nodes || [],
          edges: generated.edges || [],
          yaml: generated.yaml || '',
          mermaid: generated.mermaid || '',
          variables: {}
        })
      });
      
      if (!saveResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to save process');
      }
      
      await loadProcesses();
      
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'completed', undefined,
        `Process created: "${decomposition.title}" with ${decomposition.subtasks.length} steps.`
      );
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to create process:', error);
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'failed', undefined,
        `Failed to create process: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingProcess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading || isWaitingResponse) return;
    
    let chatId = activeChatId;
    if (!chatId) {
      addChat();
      const newState = useChatStore.getState();
      chatId = newState.activeChatId;
    }
    
    const command = inputValue;
    setInputValue('');
    
    addMessage(chatId!, {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
      role: 'user',
      content: command,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'completed',
    });
    
    setIsWaitingResponse(true);
    
    try {
      await sendMessage(command);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Send command error:', error);
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      addMessage(chatId!, {
        id: `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  const handleTaskClick = (taskId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
  };
  
  const handleNavigateToTasks = (taskId?: string) => {
    if (taskId) {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
    } else {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks' });
    }
  };

  const handleProcessClick = (processId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer', search: { processId } });
  };

  const handleNavigateToProcesses = () => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer' });
  };

  const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {
    await executeCommand(cmd, selectedProjectId);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', width: '100%', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '8px 16px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('chat')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'chat' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'chat' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            CHAT
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('terminal')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'terminal' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'terminal' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            TERMINAL
          </button>
        </div>
        
        {mode === 'terminal' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)' }}>Project:</span>
            <div style={{ minWidth: '200px' }}>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'chat' ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <ChatHistory />
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 28 }}>
              {messages.map(msg => (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: msg.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
                    marginBottom: 16,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      maxWidth: '70%',
                      padding: '12px 16px',
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      background: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' 
                        : 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'white' 
                        : 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>{msg.content}</div>
                    {msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.status === 'completed' && (
                      <div 
                        onClick={() => createProcessFromDecomposition(msg.content)}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: 6,
                          marginTop: 12,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          opacity: 0.7,
                          transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1'; }}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.7'; }}
                      >
                        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                          <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" />
                        </svg>
                        <span>Create Process</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {isWaitingResponse && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                  <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.2s' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.4s' }}>●</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div style={{ padding: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <textarea
                    style={{
                      width: '100%',
                      padding: 12,
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                      background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      resize: 'none',
                      outline: 'none',
                    }}
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Enter command or requirement..."
                    value={inputValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={isLoading || isWaitingResponse}
                  style={{
                    padding: '10px 20px',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    border: 'none',
                    background: 'var(--k-accent-ocean)',
                    color: 'white',
                    cursor: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    opacity: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 0.6 : 1,
                  }}
                >
                  Send
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="chat"
            tasks={tasks}
            processes={processes}
            components={components}
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <WorkspaceIDE 
            onCommand={handleTerminalCommand} 
            selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} 
            output={output} 
            isExecuting={isExecuting} 
            tasks={tasks} 
            processes={processes} 
            components={components} 
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick} 
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick} 
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks} 
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses} 
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useChatStore } from '../features/chat/store/chatStore';
import { getTaskStatus, getTasks } from '../batuta';
import { ChatHistory } from '../features/chat/components/ChatHistory';
import { UnifiedRightPanelIDE } from '../features/ide/UnifiedRightPanelIDE';
import { useChatDirect } from '../features/chat/hooks/useChatDirect';
import { decomposeTask } from '../batuta/decompose';
import { WorkspaceIDE, useProjects } from '../features/ide';
import { useTerminalCommand } from '../features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand';

type TerminalMode = 'chat' | 'terminal';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed';
}

interface Process {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'generated' | 'executing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  nodes?: any[];
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Component {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  version: string;
}

export const TerminalPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { chats, activeChatId, addChat, addMessage, updateMessageStatus } = useChatStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TerminalMode>('chat');
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [tasksTab, setTasksTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [processTab, setProcessTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [components, setComponents] = useState<Component[]>([]);
  const [isCreatingProcess, setIsCreatingProcess] = useState(false);
  const [isWaitingResponse, setIsWaitingResponse] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const { sendMessage, isLoading, error: chatError, messages: directMessages } = useChatDirect();
  const { selectedProjectId, selectProject } = useProjects();
  const { executeCommand, output, isExecuting, clearOutput } = useTerminalCommand();
  const processedMessageIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  const currentChat = chats.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);
  const messages = currentChat?.messages || [];

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const loadTasks = async () => {
    try {
      const batutaTasks = await getTasks();
      const mappedTasks: Task[] = batutaTasks
        .filter(t => t.status !== 'archived')
        .map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          description: t.description,
          status: t.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : (t.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'pending')
        }));
      setTasks(mappedTasks);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load tasks:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/process/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setProcesses(data.processes || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadComponents = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/components/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setComponents(data.components || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load components:', error);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
    loadProcesses();
    loadComponents();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadTasks();
      loadProcesses();
      loadComponents();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChatId) return;
    
    directMessages.forEach(msg => {
      if (!processedMessageIds.current.has(msg.id)) {
        processedMessageIds.current.add(msg.id);
        
        if (msg.role === 'user') {
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'user',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        } else if (msg.role === 'agent') {
          setIsWaitingResponse(false);
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }, [directMessages, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chatError && activeChatId) {
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      const errorMessageId = `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
      addMessage(activeChatId, {
        id: errorMessageId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${chatError}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  }, [chatError, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  const createProcessFromDecomposition = async (taskText: string) => {
    if (!activeChatId || isCreatingProcess) return;
    
    setIsCreatingProcess(true);
    const processingMsgId = `process_gen_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
    addMessage(activeChatId, {
      id: processingMsgId,
      role: 'assistant',
      content: 'Creating process from decomposition...',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'in_progress',
    });
    
    try {
      const decomposition = await decomposeTask(
        taskText.substring(0, 100),
        taskText
      );
      
      const subtasksText = decomposition.subtasks
        .map((st, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${st.title}: ${st.description}`)
        .join('\n');
      
      const generationPrompt = `Create BPMN process based on these steps:\n\n${subtasksText}\n\nProcess name: ${decomposition.title}`;

      const generateResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/design', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          requirement: generationPrompt,
          project_id: null 
        }),
      });
      
      if (!generateResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
      }
      
      const generated = await generateResponse.json();
      
      const saveResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: decomposition.title,
          description: `Generated from: ${taskText.substring(0, 200)}`,
          nodes: generated.nodes || [],
          edges: generated.edges || [],
          yaml: generated.yaml || '',
          mermaid: generated.mermaid || '',
          variables: {}
        })
      });
      
      if (!saveResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to save process');
      }
      
      await loadProcesses();
      
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'completed', undefined,
        `Process created: "${decomposition.title}" with ${decomposition.subtasks.length} steps.`
      );
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to create process:', error);
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'failed', undefined,
        `Failed to create process: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingProcess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading || isWaitingResponse) return;
    
    let chatId = activeChatId;
    if (!chatId) {
      addChat();
      const newState = useChatStore.getState();
      chatId = newState.activeChatId;
    }
    
    const command = inputValue;
    setInputValue('');
    
    addMessage(chatId!, {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
      role: 'user',
      content: command,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'completed',
    });
    
    setIsWaitingResponse(true);
    
    try {
      await sendMessage(command);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Send command error:', error);
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      addMessage(chatId!, {
        id: `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  const handleTaskClick = (taskId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
  };
  
  const handleNavigateToTasks = (taskId?: string) => {
    if (taskId) {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
    } else {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks' });
    }
  };

  const handleProcessClick = (processId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer', search: { processId } });
  };

  const handleNavigateToProcesses = () => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer' });
  };

  const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {
    await executeCommand(cmd, selectedProjectId);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', width: '100%', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '8px 16px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('chat')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'chat' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'chat' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            CHAT
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('terminal')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'terminal' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'terminal' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            TERMINAL
          </button>
        </div>
        
        {mode === 'terminal' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)' }}>Project:</span>
            <div style={{ minWidth: '200px' }}>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'chat' ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <ChatHistory />
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 28 }}>
              {messages.map(msg => (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: msg.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
                    marginBottom: 16,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      maxWidth: '70%',
                      padding: '12px 16px',
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      background: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' 
                        : 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'white' 
                        : 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>{msg.content}</div>
                    {msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.status === 'completed' && (
                      <div 
                        onClick={() => createProcessFromDecomposition(msg.content)}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: 6,
                          marginTop: 12,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          opacity: 0.7,
                          transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1'; }}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.7'; }}
                      >
                        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                          <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" />
                        </svg>
                        <span>Create Process</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {isWaitingResponse && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                  <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.2s' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.4s' }}>●</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div style={{ padding: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <textarea
                    style={{
                      width: '100%',
                      padding: 12,
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                      background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      resize: 'none',
                      outline: 'none',
                    }}
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Enter command or requirement..."
                    value={inputValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={isLoading || isWaitingResponse}
                  style={{
                    padding: '10px 20px',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    border: 'none',
                    background: 'var(--k-accent-ocean)',
                    color: 'white',
                    cursor: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    opacity: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 0.6 : 1,
                  }}
                >
                  Send
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="chat"
            tasks={tasks}
            processes={processes}
            components={components}
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <WorkspaceIDE 
            onCommand={handleTerminalCommand} 
            selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} 
            output={output} 
            isExecuting={isExecuting} 
            tasks={tasks} 
            processes={processes} 
            components={components} 
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick} 
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick} 
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks} 
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses} 
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

---

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Вот обновленный файл с интегрированным `SafeTerminal` вместо `WorkspaceIDE` в режиме терминала. Вся существующая функциональность CHAT режима сохранена.

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/pages/TerminalPage.tsx << 'EOF'
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useChatStore } from '../features/chat/store/chatStore';
import { getTaskStatus, getTasks } from '../batuta';
import { ChatHistory } from '../features/chat/components/ChatHistory';
import { UnifiedRightPanelIDE } from '../features/ide/UnifiedRightPanelIDE';
import { useChatDirect } from '../features/chat/hooks/useChatDirect';
import { decomposeTask } from '../batuta/decompose';
import { WorkspaceIDE, useProjects } from '../features/ide';
import { SafeTerminal } from '../components/Terminal/SafeTerminal';

type TerminalMode = 'chat' | 'terminal';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed';
}

interface Process {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'generated' | 'executing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  nodes?: any[];
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Component {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  version: string;
}

export const TerminalPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { chats, activeChatId, addChat, addMessage, updateMessageStatus } = useChatStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TerminalMode>('chat');
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [tasksTab, setTasksTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [processTab, setProcessTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [components, setComponents] = useState<Component[]>([]);
  const [isCreatingProcess, setIsCreatingProcess] = useState(false);
  const [isWaitingResponse, setIsWaitingResponse] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const { sendMessage, isLoading, error: chatError, messages: directMessages } = useChatDirect();
  const { selectedProjectId, selectProject } = useProjects();
  const processedMessageIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  const currentChat = chats.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);
  const messages = currentChat?.messages || [];

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const loadTasks = async () => {
    try {
      const batutaTasks = await getTasks();
      const mappedTasks: Task[] = batutaTasks
        .filter(t => t.status !== 'archived')
        .map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          description: t.description,
          status: t.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : (t.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'pending')
        }));
      setTasks(mappedTasks);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load tasks:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/process/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setProcesses(data.processes || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadComponents = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/components/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setComponents(data.components || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load components:', error);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
    loadProcesses();
    loadComponents();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadTasks();
      loadProcesses();
      loadComponents();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChatId) return;
    
    directMessages.forEach(msg => {
      if (!processedMessageIds.current.has(msg.id)) {
        processedMessageIds.current.add(msg.id);
        
        if (msg.role === 'user') {
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'user',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        } else if (msg.role === 'agent') {
          setIsWaitingResponse(false);
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }, [directMessages, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chatError && activeChatId) {
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      const errorMessageId = `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
      addMessage(activeChatId, {
        id: errorMessageId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${chatError}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  }, [chatError, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  const createProcessFromDecomposition = async (taskText: string) => {
    if (!activeChatId || isCreatingProcess) return;
    
    setIsCreatingProcess(true);
    const processingMsgId = `process_gen_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
    addMessage(activeChatId, {
      id: processingMsgId,
      role: 'assistant',
      content: 'Creating process from decomposition...',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'in_progress',
    });
    
    try {
      const decomposition = await decomposeTask(
        taskText.substring(0, 100),
        taskText
      );
      
      const subtasksText = decomposition.subtasks
        .map((st, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${st.title}: ${st.description}`)
        .join('\n');
      
      const generationPrompt = `Create BPMN process based on these steps:\n\n${subtasksText}\n\nProcess name: ${decomposition.title}`;

      const generateResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/design', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          requirement: generationPrompt,
          project_id: null 
        }),
      });
      
      if (!generateResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
      }
      
      const generated = await generateResponse.json();
      
      const saveResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: decomposition.title,
          description: `Generated from: ${taskText.substring(0, 200)}`,
          nodes: generated.nodes || [],
          edges: generated.edges || [],
          yaml: generated.yaml || '',
          mermaid: generated.mermaid || '',
          variables: {}
        })
      });
      
      if (!saveResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to save process');
      }
      
      await loadProcesses();
      
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'completed', undefined,
        `Process created: "${decomposition.title}" with ${decomposition.subtasks.length} steps.`
      );
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to create process:', error);
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'failed', undefined,
        `Failed to create process: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingProcess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading || isWaitingResponse) return;
    
    let chatId = activeChatId;
    if (!chatId) {
      addChat();
      const newState = useChatStore.getState();
      chatId = newState.activeChatId;
    }
    
    const command = inputValue;
    setInputValue('');
    
    addMessage(chatId!, {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
      role: 'user',
      content: command,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'completed',
    });
    
    setIsWaitingResponse(true);
    
    try {
      await sendMessage(command);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Send command error:', error);
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      addMessage(chatId!, {
        id: `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  const handleTaskClick = (taskId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
  };
  
  const handleNavigateToTasks = (taskId?: string) => {
    if (taskId) {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
    } else {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks' });
    }
  };

  const handleProcessClick = (processId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer', search: { processId } });
  };

  const handleNavigateToProcesses = () => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer' });
  };

  const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {
    // Для совместимости со старым интерфейсом
    console.log('Terminal command:', cmd);
  };

  // Получаем путь к проекту для терминала
  const projectPath = selectedProjectId ? `/projects/${selectedProjectId}` : undefined;

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', width: '100%', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '8px 16px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('chat')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'chat' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'chat' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            CHAT
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('terminal')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'terminal' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'terminal' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            TERMINAL
          </button>
        </div>
        
        {mode === 'terminal' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)' }}>Project:</span>
            <div style={{ minWidth: '200px' }}>
              <select 
                value={selectedProjectId || ''} 
                onChange={(e) => selectProject(e.target.value || null)}
                style={{
                  padding: '4px 8px',
                  borderRadius: '4px',
                  background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)',
                  color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                  border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                  fontSize: '12px'
                }}
              >
                <option value="">None</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'chat' ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <ChatHistory />
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 28 }}>
              {messages.map(msg => (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: msg.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
                    marginBottom: 16,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      maxWidth: '70%',
                      padding: '12px 16px',
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      background: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' 
                        : 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'white' 
                        : 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>{msg.content}</div>
                    {msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.status === 'completed' && (
                      <div 
                        onClick={() => createProcessFromDecomposition(msg.content)}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: 6,
                          marginTop: 12,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          opacity: 0.7,
                          transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1'; }}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.7'; }}
                      >
                        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                          <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" />
                        </svg>
                        <span>Create Process</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {isWaitingResponse && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                  <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.2s' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.4s' }}>●</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div style={{ padding: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <textarea
                    style={{
                      width: '100%',
                      padding: 12,
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                      background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      resize: 'none',
                      outline: 'none',
                    }}
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Enter command or requirement..."
                    value={inputValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={isLoading || isWaitingResponse}
                  style={{
                    padding: '10px 20px',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    border: 'none',
                    background: 'var(--k-accent-ocean)',
                    color: 'white',
                    cursor: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    opacity: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 0.6 : 1,
                  }}
                >
                  Send
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="chat"
            tasks={tasks}
            processes={processes}
            components={components}
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0, flexDirection: 'column' }}>
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', minHeight: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', background: '#000' }}>
              <SafeTerminal 
                cwd={projectPath}
                onCommandExecuted={(cmd, result) => {
                  console.log('Command executed:', cmd, result);
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0, borderLeft: '1px solid var(--k-border)' }}>
              <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="terminal"
                tasks={tasks}
                processes={processes}
                components={components}
                onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
                onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
                onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
                onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### 3. **Как должен работать TERMINAL режим:**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  [CHAT]  [TERMINAL]                          Project: [v]  │

│                                                             │
│  $ ls -la                      ← SAFE (выполняется сразу)  │

│                                                             │
│  $ rm test.txt                 ← WARNING (модальное окно)  │
│  ⚠️ Требуется подтверждение                                 │
│  [Отмена] [Подтвердить]                                    │
│                                                             │
│  $ sudo apt update             ← DANGEROUS (создает HITL)  │
│  📋 Создана задача: cmd_20240522_143022                    │
│  Используйте /approve <id> или /reject <id>               │
│                                                             │
│  $ /pending                    ← Список ожидающих задач    │
│  cmd_20240522_143022: sudo apt update (dangerous)         │
│                                                             │
│  $ /approve cmd_20240522_143022  ← Подтверждение          │
│  ✅ Команда подтверждена                                   │
│                                                             │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  $ _                                                        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 👤 **Kirill**

(.venv) kirillkravcov@Mac UniversalCreativeHub % cat /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand.ts
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface CommandResult {
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
  exit_code: number;
}

export const useTerminalCommand = () => {
  const [isExecuting, setIsExecuting] = useState(false);
  const [output, setOutput] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const getProjectPath = async (projectId: string | null): Promise<string> => {
    if (!projectId) {
      return '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
    }
    
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/v1/projects/${projectId}`);
      if (response.ok) {
        const project = await response.json();
        return project.source_path || '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to get project path:', error);
    }
    
    return '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
  };

  const executeCommand = useCallback(async (command: string, projectId: string | null): Promise<CommandResult | null> => {
    setIsExecuting(true);
    setOutput(prev => [...prev, `$ ${command}`]);
    
    const cwd = await getProjectPath(projectId);
    
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/execute/command', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          command,
          cwd
        })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Command failed: ${response.status}`);
      }
      
      const result = await response.json();
      if (result.stdout) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
      }
      if (result.stderr) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
      }
      
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMsg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
      setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${errorMsg}`]);
      return null;
    } finally {
      setIsExecuting(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const clearOutput = useCallback(() => {
    setOutput([]);
  }, []);

  return {
    executeCommand,
    output,
    isExecuting,
    clearOutput
  };
};

- У нас есть `/api/v1/execute/safe` (безопасное выполнение)

Модифицировать `useTerminalCommand.ts`, чтобы он:

2. Для **SAFE** → вызывал `/safe` (а не `/command`)
3. Для **WARNING** → показывал модальное окно, затем `/safe` с `approved: true`

**Действие 21: Модифицируем `useTerminalCommand.ts` с полной интеграцией безопасности**

Создам полную версию:

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand.ts << 'EOF'
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface CommandResult {
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
  exit_code: number;
  safety_level?: string;
}

interface CommandAnalysis {
  command: string;
  safety_level: 'safe' | 'warning' | 'dangerous';
  requires_approval: boolean;
  matches: string[];
  is_dangerous: boolean;
  is_warning: boolean;
  is_safe: boolean;
}

interface PendingApproval {
  execution_id: string;
  command: string;
  safety_level: string;
  matches: string[];
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'executed';
}

export const useTerminalCommand = () => {
  const [isExecuting, setIsExecuting] = useState(false);
  const [output, setOutput] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [pendingApprovals, setPendingApprovals] = useState<PendingApproval[]>([]);
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [pendingCommand, setPendingCommand] = useState<{ command: string; analysis: CommandAnalysis } | null>(null);
  const [modalResolve, setModalResolve] = useState<((value: boolean) => void) | null>(null);

  const getProjectPath = async (projectId: string | null): Promise<string> => {
    if (!projectId) {
      return '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
    }
    
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/v1/projects/${projectId}`);
      if (response.ok) {
        const project = await response.json();
        return project.source_path || '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to get project path:', error);
    }
    
    return '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub';
  };

  // Анализ команды
  const analyzeCommand = async (command: string): Promise<CommandAnalysis | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/analyze', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Analysis failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  };

  // Выполнение команды через безопасный API
  const executeSafeCommand = async (command: string, cwd: string, approved: boolean = false): Promise<CommandResult | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/safe', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command, cwd, approved })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        const error = await response.json();
        throw new Error(error.detail || 'Command failed');
      }
      
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Execution failed:', error);
      throw error;
    }
  };

  // Запрос подтверждения для опасной команды
  const requestApproval = async (command: string, cwd: string): Promise<PendingApproval | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/request-approval', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command, cwd })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      
      const data = await response.json();
      const approval: PendingApproval = {
        execution_id: data.execution_id,
        command: data.command,
        safety_level: data.safety_level,
        matches: data.matches,
        status: 'pending'
      };
      
      setPendingApprovals(prev => [...prev, approval]);
      return approval;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Request approval failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  };

  // Подтверждение команды
  const approveCommand = async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/approve/${execution_id}`, {
        method: 'POST'
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      
      const result = await response.json();
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'executed' } : p
      ));
      
      if (result.stdout) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
      }
      if (result.stderr) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
      }
      
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Approve failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  };

  // Отклонение команды
  const rejectCommand = async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/reject/${execution_id}`, {
        method: 'POST'
      });
      
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'rejected' } : p
      ));
      
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Reject failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  };

  // Показать модальное окно и ждать ответа
  const showConfirmationModal = (command: string, analysis: CommandAnalysis): Promise<boolean> => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      setPendingCommand({ command, analysis });
      setModalResolve(() => resolve);
      setShowModal(true);
    });
  };

  // Основная функция выполнения команды
  const executeCommand = useCallback(async (command: string, projectId: string | null): Promise<CommandResult | null> => {
    setIsExecuting(true);
    setOutput(prev => [...prev, `$ ${command}`]);
    
    const cwd = await getProjectPath(projectId);
    
    // Обработка внутренних команд
    if (command === '/help') {
      setOutput(prev => [...prev, 
        'Доступные команды:',
        '  /help        - показать эту справку',
        '  /clear       - очистить терминал',
        '  /approve <id> - подтвердить выполнение команды',
        '  /reject <id>  - отклонить выполнение команды',
        '  /pending     - показать ожидающие подтверждения'
      ]);
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command === '/clear') {
      setOutput([]);
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/approve ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await approveCommand(id);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `✅ Команда ${id} подтверждена`]);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, '❌ Укажите ID команды: /approve <id>']);
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/reject ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await rejectCommand(id);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Команда ${id} отклонена`]);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, '❌ Укажите ID команды: /reject <id>']);
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command === '/pending') {
      if (pendingApprovals.length === 0) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, 'Нет ожидающих подтверждения команд']);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, 'Ожидающие подтверждения команды:']);
        pendingApprovals.forEach(p => {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `  ${p.execution_id}: ${p.command} (${p.safety_level})`]);
        });
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    try {
      // 1. Анализируем команду
      const analysis = await analyzeCommand(command);
      if (!analysis) {
        throw new Error('Не удалось проанализировать команду');
      }
      
      // 2. В зависимости от уровня безопасности
      if (analysis.is_safe) {
        // SAFE - выполняем сразу
        const result = await executeSafeCommand(command, cwd, false);
        if (result) {
          if (result.stdout) setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
          if (result.stderr) setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
        }
        return result;
      } 
      else if (analysis.is_warning) {
        // WARNING - показываем модальное окно
        const approved = await showConfirmationModal(command, analysis);
        if (approved) {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `⚠️ Команда требует подтверждения: ${analysis.matches.join(', ')}`]);
          const result = await executeSafeCommand(command, cwd, true);
          if (result) {
            if (result.stdout) setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
            if (result.stderr) setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
          }
          return result;
        } else {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Команда отклонена: ${command}`]);
          return null;
        }
      } 
      else if (analysis.is_dangerous) {
        // DANGEROUS - создаем HITL задачу
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `⚠️ ОПАСНАЯ КОМАНДА! Создаю задачу для подтверждения...`]);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   Причина: ${analysis.matches.join(', ')}`]);
        
        const approval = await requestApproval(command, cwd);
        if (approval) {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `📋 Создана задача подтверждения: ${approval.execution_id}`]);
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   Используйте /approve ${approval.execution_id} для выполнения`]);
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   или /reject ${approval.execution_id} для отклонения`]);
        } else {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Не удалось создать задачу подтверждения`]);
        }
        return null;
      }
      
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMsg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
      setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${errorMsg}`]);
      return null;
    } finally {
      setIsExecuting(false);
    }
  }, [pendingApprovals]);

  const clearOutput = useCallback(() => {
    setOutput([]);
  }, []);

  const confirmCommand = useCallback((approved: boolean) => {
    if (modalResolve) {
      modalResolve(approved);
    }
    setShowModal(false);
    setPendingCommand(null);
    setModalResolve(null);
  }, [modalResolve]);

  const getPendingCommand = useCallback(() => pendingCommand, [pendingCommand]);
  const getShowModal = useCallback(() => showModal, [showModal]);

  return {
    executeCommand,
    output,
    isExecuting,
    clearOutput,
    confirmCommand,
    getPendingCommand,
    getShowModal,
    pendingApprovals
  };
};
```

```bash
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/analyze', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Analysis failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  const executeSafeCommand = useCallback(async (command: string, cwd: string, approved: boolean = false): Promise<CommandResult | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/safe', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command, cwd, approved })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) {
        const error = await response.json();
        throw new Error(error.detail || 'Command failed');
      }
      
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Execution failed:', error);
      throw error;
    }
  }, []);

  const requestApproval = useCallback(async (command: string, cwd: string): Promise<PendingApproval | null> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/request-approval', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ command, cwd })
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      
      const data = await response.json();
      const approval: PendingApproval = {
        execution_id: data.execution_id,
        command: data.command,
        safety_level: data.safety_level,
        matches: data.matches,
        status: 'pending'
      };
      
      setPendingApprovals(prev => [...prev, approval]);
      return approval;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Request approval failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  const approveCommand = useCallback(async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/approve/${execution_id}`, {
        method: 'POST'
      });
      
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      
      const result = await response.json();
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'executed' } : p
      ));
      
      if (result.stdout) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
      }
      if (result.stderr) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
      }
      
      return result;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Approve failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  const rejectCommand = useCallback(async (execution_id: string): Promise<any> => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`http://localhost:8010/api/v1/execute/reject/${execution_id}`, {
        method: 'POST'
      });
      
      setPendingApprovals(prev => prev.map(p => 
        p.execution_id === execution_id ? { ...p, status: 'rejected' } : p
      ));
      
      return await response.json();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Reject failed:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  const showConfirmationModal = useCallback((command: string, analysis: CommandAnalysis): Promise<boolean> => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      setPendingCommand({ command, analysis });
      setModalResolve(() => resolve);
      setShowModal(true);
    });
  }, []);

  const confirmCommand = useCallback((approved: boolean) => {
    if (modalResolve) {
      modalResolve(approved);
    }
    setShowModal(false);
    setPendingCommand(null);
    setModalResolve(null);
  }, [modalResolve]);

  const executeCommand = useCallback(async (command: string, projectId: string | null): Promise<CommandResult | null> => {
    setIsExecuting(true);
    setOutput(prev => [...prev, `$ ${command}`]);
    
    const cwd = await getProjectPath(projectId);
    
    // Обработка внутренних команд
    if (command === '/help') {
      setOutput(prev => [...prev, 
        'Доступные команды:',
        '  /help        - показать эту справку',
        '  /clear       - очистить терминал',
        '  /approve <id> - подтвердить выполнение команды',
        '  /reject <id>  - отклонить выполнение команды',
        '  /pending     - показать ожидающие подтверждения'
      ]);
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command === '/clear') {
      setOutput([]);
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/approve ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await approveCommand(id);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `✅ Команда ${id} подтверждена`]);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, '❌ Укажите ID команды: /approve <id>']);
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command.startsWith('/reject ')) {
      const id = command.split(' ')[1];
      if (id) {
        await rejectCommand(id);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Команда ${id} отклонена`]);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, '❌ Укажите ID команды: /reject <id>']);
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    if (command === '/pending') {
      if (pendingApprovals.length === 0) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, 'Нет ожидающих подтверждения команд']);
      } else {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, 'Ожидающие подтверждения команды:']);
        pendingApprovals.forEach(p => {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `  ${p.execution_id}: ${p.command} (${p.safety_level})`]);
        });
      }
      setIsExecuting(false);
      return null;
    }
    
    try {
      const analysis = await analyzeCommand(command);
      if (!analysis) {
        throw new Error('Не удалось проанализировать команду');
      }
      
      if (analysis.is_safe) {
        const result = await executeSafeCommand(command, cwd, false);
        if (result) {
          if (result.stdout) setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
          if (result.stderr) setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
        }
        return result;
      } 
      else if (analysis.is_warning) {
        const approved = await showConfirmationModal(command, analysis);
        if (approved) {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `⚠️ Команда требует подтверждения: ${analysis.matches.join(', ')}`]);
          const result = await executeSafeCommand(command, cwd, true);
          if (result) {
            if (result.stdout) setOutput(prev => [...prev, result.stdout]);
            if (result.stderr) setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${result.stderr}`]);
          }
          return result;
        } else {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Команда отклонена: ${command}`]);
          return null;
        }
      } 
      else if (analysis.is_dangerous) {
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `⚠️ ОПАСНАЯ КОМАНДА! Создаю задачу для подтверждения...`]);
        setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   Причина: ${analysis.matches.join(', ')}`]);
        
        const approval = await requestApproval(command, cwd);
        if (approval) {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `📋 Создана задача подтверждения: ${approval.execution_id}`]);
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   Используйте /approve ${approval.execution_id} для выполнения`]);
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `   или /reject ${approval.execution_id} для отклонения`]);
        } else {
          setOutput(prev => [...prev, `❌ Не удалось создать задачу подтверждения`]);
        }
        return null;
      }
      
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMsg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
      setOutput(prev => [...prev, `Error: ${errorMsg}`]);
      return null;
    } finally {
      setIsExecuting(false);
    }
  }, [getProjectPath, analyzeCommand, executeSafeCommand, showConfirmationModal, requestApproval, approveCommand, rejectCommand, pendingApprovals]);

  const clearOutput = useCallback(() => {
    setOutput([]);
  }, []);

  const getPendingCommand = useCallback(() => pendingCommand, [pendingCommand]);
  const getShowModal = useCallback(() => showModal, [showModal]);

  return {
    executeCommand,
    output,
    isExecuting,
    clearOutput,
    confirmCommand,
    getPendingCommand,
    getShowModal,
    pendingApprovals
  };
};
EOF

echo "✅ useTerminalCommand.ts исправлен"
```
