import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useChatStore } from '../features/chat/store/chatStore';
import { getTaskStatus, getTasks } from '../batuta';
import { ChatHistory } from '../features/chat/components/ChatHistory';
import { UnifiedRightPanelIDE } from '../features/ide/UnifiedRightPanelIDE';
import { useChatDirect } from '../features/chat/hooks/useChatDirect';
import { decomposeTask } from '../batuta/decompose';
import { WorkspaceIDE, useProjects } from '../features/ide';
import { useTerminalCommand } from '../features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand';

type TerminalMode = 'chat' | 'terminal';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed';
}

interface Process {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'generated' | 'executing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  nodes?: any[];
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Component {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  version: string;
}

export const TerminalPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { chats, activeChatId, addChat, addMessage, updateMessageStatus } = useChatStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TerminalMode>('chat');
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [tasksTab, setTasksTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [processTab, setProcessTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [components, setComponents] = useState<Component[]>([]);
  const [isCreatingProcess, setIsCreatingProcess] = useState(false);
  const [isWaitingResponse, setIsWaitingResponse] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const { sendMessage, isLoading, error: chatError, messages: directMessages } = useChatDirect();
  const { selectedProjectId, selectProject } = useProjects();
  const { 
    executeCommand, 
    output, 
    isExecuting, 
    clearOutput,
    confirmCommand,
    getPendingCommand,
    getShowModal
  } = useTerminalCommand();
  const processedMessageIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  const currentChat = chats.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);
  const messages = currentChat?.messages || [];

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const loadTasks = async () => {
    try {
      const batutaTasks = await getTasks();
      const mappedTasks: Task[] = batutaTasks
        .filter(t => t.status !== 'archived')
        .map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          description: t.description,
          status: t.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : (t.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'pending')
        }));
      setTasks(mappedTasks);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load tasks:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/process/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setProcesses(data.processes || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadComponents = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/components/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setComponents(data.components || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load components:', error);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
    loadProcesses();
    loadComponents();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadTasks();
      loadProcesses();
      loadComponents();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChatId) return;
    
    directMessages.forEach(msg => {
      if (!processedMessageIds.current.has(msg.id)) {
        processedMessageIds.current.add(msg.id);
        
        if (msg.role === 'user') {
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'user',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        } else if (msg.role === 'agent') {
          setIsWaitingResponse(false);
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }, [directMessages, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (chatError && activeChatId) {
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      const errorMessageId = `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
      addMessage(activeChatId, {
        id: errorMessageId,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${chatError}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  }, [chatError, activeChatId, addMessage]);

  const createProcessFromDecomposition = async (taskText: string) => {
    if (!activeChatId || isCreatingProcess) return;
    
    setIsCreatingProcess(true);
    const processingMsgId = `process_gen_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
    addMessage(activeChatId, {
      id: processingMsgId,
      role: 'assistant',
      content: 'Creating process from decomposition...',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'in_progress',
    });
    
    try {
      const decomposition = await decomposeTask(
        taskText.substring(0, 100),
        taskText
      );
      
      const subtasksText = decomposition.subtasks
        .map((st, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${st.title}: ${st.description}`)
        .join('\n');
      
      const generationPrompt = `Create BPMN process based on these steps:\n\n${subtasksText}\n\nProcess name: ${decomposition.title}`;

      const generateResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/design', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          requirement: generationPrompt,
          project_id: null 
        }),
      });
      
      if (!generateResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
      }
      
      const generated = await generateResponse.json();
      
      const saveResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: decomposition.title,
          description: `Generated from: ${taskText.substring(0, 200)}`,
          nodes: generated.nodes || [],
          edges: generated.edges || [],
          yaml: generated.yaml || '',
          mermaid: generated.mermaid || '',
          variables: {}
        })
      });
      
      if (!saveResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to save process');
      }
      
      await loadProcesses();
      
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'completed', undefined,
        `Process created: "${decomposition.title}" with ${decomposition.subtasks.length} steps.`
      );
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to create process:', error);
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'failed', undefined,
        `Failed to create process: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingProcess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading || isWaitingResponse) return;
    
    let chatId = activeChatId;
    if (!chatId) {
      addChat();
      const newState = useChatStore.getState();
      chatId = newState.activeChatId;
    }
    
    const command = inputValue;
    setInputValue('');
    
    addMessage(chatId!, {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
      role: 'user',
      content: command,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'completed',
    });
    
    setIsWaitingResponse(true);
    
    try {
      await sendMessage(command);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Send command error:', error);
      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      addMessage(chatId!, {
        id: `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  const handleTaskClick = (taskId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
  };
  
  const handleNavigateToTasks = (taskId?: string) => {
    if (taskId) {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks', search: { taskId } });
    } else {
      navigate({ to: '/tasks' });
    }
  };

  const handleProcessClick = (processId: string) => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer', search: { processId } });
  };

  const handleNavigateToProcesses = () => {
    navigate({ to: '/process-designer' });
  };

  const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {
    await executeCommand(cmd, selectedProjectId);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', height: '100%', width: '100%', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        padding: '8px 16px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
        background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('chat')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'chat' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'chat' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            CHAT
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode('terminal')}
            style={{
              padding: '6px 16px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              background: mode === 'terminal' ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' : 'transparent',
              color: mode === 'terminal' ? 'white' : 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontWeight: 500
            }}
          >
            TERMINAL
          </button>
        </div>
        
        {mode === 'terminal' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)' }}>Project:</span>
            <div style={{ minWidth: '200px' }}>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === 'chat' ? (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <ChatHistory />
          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minWidth: 0 }}>
            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 28 }}>
              {messages.map(msg => (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: msg.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
                    marginBottom: 16,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      maxWidth: '70%',
                      padding: '12px 16px',
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      background: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' 
                        : 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: msg.role === 'user' 
                        ? 'white' 
                        : 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>{msg.content}</div>
                    {msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.status === 'completed' && (
                      <div 
                        onClick={() => createProcessFromDecomposition(msg.content)}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: 6,
                          marginTop: 12,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          opacity: 0.7,
                          transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1'; }}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.7'; }}
                      >
                        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                          <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" />
                        </svg>
                        <span>Create Process</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {isWaitingResponse && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                  <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.2s' }}>●</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.4s' }}>●</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div style={{ padding: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)' }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <textarea
                    style={{
                      width: '100%',
                      padding: 12,
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                      background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                      color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      resize: 'none',
                      outline: 'none',
                    }}
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Enter command or requirement..."
                    value={inputValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={isLoading || isWaitingResponse}
                  style={{
                    padding: '10px 20px',
                    borderRadius: 8,
                    border: 'none',
                    background: 'var(--k-accent-ocean)',
                    color: 'white',
                    cursor: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                    opacity: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 0.6 : 1,
                  }}
                >
                  Send
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0 }}>
          <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="chat"
            tasks={tasks}
            processes={processes}
            components={components}
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
          <WorkspaceIDE 
            onCommand={handleTerminalCommand} 
            selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} 
            output={output} 
            isExecuting={isExecuting} 
            tasks={tasks} 
            processes={processes} 
            components={components} 
            onTaskClick={handleTaskClick} 
            onProcessClick={handleProcessClick} 
            onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks} 
            onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses} 
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

```tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useChatStore } from '../features/chat/store/chatStore';
import { getTaskStatus, getTasks } from '../batuta';
import { ChatHistory } from '../features/chat/components/ChatHistory';
import { UnifiedRightPanelIDE } from '../features/ide/UnifiedRightPanelIDE';
import { useChatDirect } from '../features/chat/hooks/useChatDirect';
import { decomposeTask } from '../batuta/decompose';
import { WorkspaceIDE, useProjects } from '../features/ide';
import { useTerminalCommand } from '../features/ide/hooks/useTerminalCommand';

type TerminalMode = 'chat' | 'terminal';

interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'completed' | 'pending' | 'failed';
}

interface Process {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'generated' | 'executing' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  nodes?: any[];
  createdAt: string;
}

interface Component {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  status: string;
  version: string;
}

export const TerminalPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { chats, activeChatId, addChat, addMessage, updateMessageStatus } = useChatStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TerminalMode>('chat');
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState('');
  const [tasksTab, setTasksTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [processTab, setProcessTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all'>('all');
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<Task[]>([]);
  const [processes, setProcesses] = useState<Process[]>([]);
  const [components, setComponents] = useState<Component[]>([]);
  const [isCreatingProcess, setIsCreatingProcess] = useState(false);
  const [isWaitingResponse, setIsWaitingResponse] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  const { sendMessage, isLoading, error: chatError, messages: directMessages } = useChatDirect();
  const { selectedProjectId, selectProject } = useProjects();
  const { 
    executeCommand, 
    output, 
    isExecuting, 
    clearOutput,
    confirmCommand,
    getPendingCommand,
    getShowModal
  } = useTerminalCommand();
  const processedMessageIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  
  const currentChat = chats.find(c => c.id === activeChatId);
  const messages = currentChat?.messages || [];

  const pendingCommandData = getPendingCommand();
  const showModal = getShowModal();

  const ConfirmationModal = () => {
    if (!showModal || !pendingCommandData) return null;
    const isDangerous = pendingCommandData.analysis.safety_level === 'dangerous';
    return (
      <div style={{
        position: 'fixed', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0,
        background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center', zIndex: 1000
      }}>
        <div style={{
          background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)', borderRadius: '12px', padding: '24px',
          maxWidth: '500px', width: '90%', border: `2px solid ${isDangerous ? '#ef4444' : '#f59e0b'}`
        }}>
          <h3 style={{ color: isDangerous ? '#ef4444' : '#f59e0b', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
            {isDangerous ? '⚠️ ОПАСНАЯ КОМАНДА' : '⚠️ Требуется подтверждение'}
          </h3>
          <div style={{ background: '#000', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '6px', fontFamily: 'monospace', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
            {pendingCommandData.command}
          </div>
          <div style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
            <strong>Причина:</strong>
            <ul style={{ marginTop: '8px', marginLeft: '20px' }}>
              {pendingCommandData.analysis.matches.map((m, i) => <li key={i}>{m}</li>)}
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
            <button onClick={() => confirmCommand(false)} style={{ padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'transparent', color: 'var(--k-text-primary)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>Отмена</button>
            <button onClick={() => confirmCommand(true)} style={{ padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: '6px', border: 'none', background: isDangerous ? '#ef4444' : '#f59e0b', color: 'white', cursor: 'pointer' }}>Подтвердить</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  const loadTasks = async () => {
    try {
      const batutaTasks = await getTasks();
      const mappedTasks: Task[] = batutaTasks
        .filter(t => t.status !== 'archived')
        .map(t => ({
          id: t.id,
          title: t.title,
          description: t.description,
          status: t.status === 'completed' ? 'completed' : (t.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'pending')
        }));
      setTasks(mappedTasks);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load tasks:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcesses = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/v1/process/list');
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        setProcesses(data.processes || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadComponents = async () => {

        const data = await response.json();
        setComponents(data.components || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load components:', error);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTasks();
    loadProcesses();
    loadComponents();
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadTasks();
      loadProcesses();
      loadComponents();
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {

            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',

          setIsWaitingResponse(false);
          addMessage(activeChatId, {
            id: msg.id,
            role: 'assistant',
            content: msg.content,
            timestamp: msg.timestamp,
            status: 'completed',

        }
      }
    });

      const errorMessageId = `error_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`;
      addMessage(activeChatId, {

    try {

      const subtasksText = decomposition.subtasks
        .map((st, idx) => `${idx + 1}. ${st.title}: ${st.description}`)
        .join('\n');
      
      const generationPrompt = `Create BPMN process based on these steps:\n\n${subtasksText}\n\nProcess name: ${decomposition.title}`;

      const generateResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/design', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
          requirement: generationPrompt,
          project_id: null 
        }),
      });
      
      if (!generateResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
      }
      
      const generated = await generateResponse.json();
      
      const saveResponse = await fetch('/api/v1/process/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: decomposition.title,
          description: `Generated from: ${taskText.substring(0, 200)}`,
          nodes: generated.nodes || [],
          edges: generated.edges || [],
          yaml: generated.yaml || '',
          mermaid: generated.mermaid || '',
          variables: {}
        })
      });
      
      if (!saveResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to save process');
      }
      
      await loadProcesses();
      
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'completed', undefined,
        `Process created: "${decomposition.title}" with ${decomposition.subtasks.length} steps.`
      );
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to create process:', error);
      updateMessageStatus(activeChatId, processingMsgId, 'failed', undefined,
        `Failed to create process: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCreatingProcess(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!inputValue.trim() || isLoading || isWaitingResponse) return;
    
    let chatId = activeChatId;
    if (!chatId) {
      addChat();
      const newState = useChatStore.getState();
      chatId = newState.activeChatId;
    }

    setInputValue('');
    
    addMessage(chatId!, {
      id: `user_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
      role: 'user',
      content: command,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'completed',
    });

    try {
      await sendMessage(command);
    } catch (error) {

      setIsWaitingResponse(false);
      addMessage(chatId!, {

        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        status: 'failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {

      handleSend();
    }

  const handleTaskClick = (taskId: string) => {

    }
  };

  };

  const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {

  return (

              <div style={{ flex: 1, overflowY: 'auto', padding: 28 }}>

                    key={msg.id}
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      justifyContent: msg.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
                      marginBottom: 16,
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        maxWidth: '70%',
                        padding: '12px 16px',
                        borderRadius: 12,
                        background: msg.role === 'user' 
                          ? 'var(--k-accent-ocean)' 
                          : 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                        color: msg.role === 'user' 
                          ? 'white' 
                          : 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                      }}
                    >
                      <div>{msg.content}</div>
                      {msg.role === 'assistant' && msg.status === 'completed' && (
                        <div 
                          onClick={() => createProcessFromDecomposition(msg.content)}
                          style={{
                            display: 'inline-flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: 6,
                            marginTop: 12,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            color: 'var(--k-text-secondary)',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            opacity: 0.7,
                            transition: 'opacity 0.2s',
                          }}
                          onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '1'; }}
                          onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.opacity = '0.7'; }}
                        >
                          <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
                            <path d="M12 5v14M5 12h14" />
                          </svg>
                          <span>Create Process</span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
                {isWaitingResponse && (
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 16 }}>
                    <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: 12, background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite' }}>●</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.2s' }}>●</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: 8, animation: 'pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite 0.4s' }}>●</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
              </div>
              
              <div style={{ padding: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--k-border)', background: 'var(--k-bg-abyss)' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                  <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                    <textarea
                      style={{
                        width: '100%',
                        padding: 12,
                        borderRadius: 8,
                        border: '1px solid var(--k-border)',
                        background: 'var(--k-bg-mid)',
                        color: 'var(--k-text-primary)',
                        fontSize: 14,
                        resize: 'none',
                        outline: 'none',
                      }}
                      rows={2}
                      placeholder="Enter command or requirement..."
                      value={inputValue}
                      onChange={(e) => setInputValue(e.target.value)}
                      onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleSend}
                    disabled={isLoading || isWaitingResponse}
                    style={{
                      padding: '10px 20px',
                      borderRadius: 8,
                      border: 'none',
                      background: 'var(--k-accent-ocean)',
                      color: 'white',
                      cursor: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                      opacity: (isLoading || isWaitingResponse) ? 0.6 : 1,
                    }}
                  >
                    Send
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div style={{ width: "320px", flexShrink: 0 }}>
            <UnifiedRightPanelIDE mode="chat"
              tasks={tasks}
              processes={processes}
              components={components}
              onTaskClick={handleTaskClick}
              onProcessClick={handleProcessClick}
              onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks}
              onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
            <WorkspaceIDE 
              onCommand={handleTerminalCommand} 
              selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId} 
              output={output} 
              isExecuting={isExecuting} 
              tasks={tasks} 
              processes={processes} 
              components={components} 
              onTaskClick={handleTaskClick} 
              onProcessClick={handleProcessClick} 
              onNavigateToTasks={handleNavigateToTasks} 
              onNavigateToProcesses={handleNavigateToProcesses} 
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
};
```

Сохрани файл и проверь в браузере. После сохранения скажи "готово".

### 3. Проверь WARNING команду (должно появиться модальное окно)
```bash
rm test.txt
```
**Ожидаемый результат:**

- При нажатии "Подтвердить" — выполнится
- При нажатии "Отмена" — отменится


- Появится ID задачи: `cmd_20240523_...`
- Инструкция как подтвердить

```bash
/pending
```
**Ожидаемый результат:** список ожидающих задач

```bash
/help
```
**Ожидаемый результат:** справка по командам

```bash
/clear
```
**Ожидаемый результат:** очистка экрана терминала

Понял. Проблема в том, что команды в терминале идут через `onCommand` в `TerminalInput`, который вызывает `handleTerminalCommand` в `TerminalPage`, который вызывает `executeCommand` из нашего хука.

Но `executeCommand` в хуке должен был обрабатывать `/approve`, `/pending` и т.д. Давай проверим, почему они не обрабатываются.

Замени на:

```tsx
const handleTerminalCommand = async (cmd: string) => {

};
```

### **Действие 2: Добавляем эндпоинт поиска с фильтрацией**

```bash
cat >> /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_components.py << 'EOF'

@router.get("/search")
async def search_components(
    q: Optional[str] = None,
    type: Optional[str] = None,
    status: Optional[str] = None,
    tag: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    offset: int = 0
):
    """
    Поиск компонентов с фильтрацией по:
    - q: поиск по имени и описанию (полнотекстовый)
    - type: skill | process | template
    - status: draft | published | deprecated
    - tag: фильтр по тегам
    - limit, offset: пагинация
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    query = "SELECT * FROM components WHERE 1=1"
    params = []
    
    if type:
        query += " AND type = ?"
        params.append(type)
    
    if status:
        query += " AND status = ?"
        params.append(status)
    
    if q:
        query += " AND (name LIKE ? OR description LIKE ?)"
        params.append(f"%{q}%")
        params.append(f"%{q}%")
    
    if tag:
        query += " AND tags LIKE ?"
        params.append(f"%{tag}%")
    
    # Сначала получим общее количество
    count_query = query.replace("SELECT *", "SELECT COUNT(*) as total")
    cursor.execute(count_query, params)
    total = cursor.fetchone()['total']
    
    # Добавляем пагинацию
    query += " ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?"
    params.extend([limit, offset])
    
    cursor.execute(query, params)
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "items": [dict(row) for row in rows],
        "total": total,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset
    }
EOF

echo "✅ Эндпоинт /search добавлен"
```

### **Действие 3: Добавляем версионирование (semver)**

```bash
cat >> /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_components.py << 'EOF'

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/promote")
async def promote_version(
    component_id: str,
    new_version: Optional[str] = None,
    bump_type: Optional[str] = "patch"  # major, minor, patch
):
    """
    Повышение версии компонента (semver)
    - bump_type: major, minor, patch
    - или указать new_version явно
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    from packaging import version
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Получаем текущий компонент
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    component = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not component:
        conn.close()
        return {"error": "Component not found"}, 404
    
    current_version = component['version'] or "1.0.0"
    
    if new_version:
        # Используем указанную версию
        next_version = new_version
    else:
        # Автоматическое повышение
        parts = current_version.split('.')
        major, minor, patch = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])
        
        if bump_type == "major":
            major += 1
            minor = 0
            patch = 0
        elif bump_type == "minor":
            minor += 1
            patch = 0
        else:  # patch
            patch += 1
        
        next_version = f"{major}.{minor}.{patch}"
    
    # Обновляем версию
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (next_version, component_id))
    
    conn.commit()
    
    # Получаем обновленный компонент
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    updated = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "old_version": current_version,
        "new_version": next_version,
        "bump_type": bump_type
    }

@router.get("/{component_id}/versions")
async def get_version_history(component_id: str):
    """
    Получить историю версий компонента
    (требуется отдельная таблица version_history)
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Проверяем существование таблицы version_history
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master 
        WHERE type='table' AND name='component_versions'
    """)
    
    if not cursor.fetchone():
        # Создаем таблицу для истории версий
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_versions (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                component_id TEXT,
                version TEXT,
                snapshot TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                created_by TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components(id)
            )
        """)
        conn.commit()
    
    # Получаем историю
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT version, snapshot, created_at 
        FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ?
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    """, (component_id,))
    
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "component_id": component_id,
        "versions": [dict(row) for row in rows]
    }

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/rollback/{target_version}")
async def rollback_version(component_id: str, target_version: str):
    """
    Откат к указанной версии
    """
    import sqlite3
    import json
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Находим snapshot целевой версии
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT snapshot FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ? AND version = ?
    """, (component_id, target_version))
    
    snapshot = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not snapshot:
        conn.close()
        return {"error": f"Version {target_version} not found"}, 404
    
    # Восстанавливаем данные из snapshot
    component_data = json.loads(snapshot['snapshot'])
    
    # Обновляем компонент
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET name = ?, description = ?, data = ?, tags = ?, version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (
        component_data.get('name'),
        component_data.get('description'),
        component_data.get('data'),
        component_data.get('tags'),
        target_version,
        component_id
    ))
    
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "rolled_back_to": target_version
    }
EOF
```

### **Действие 5: Добавляем интеграцию с Knowledge Base (векторный поиск)**

```bash
cat >> /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_components.py << 'EOF'

@router.post("/search/semantic")
async def semantic_search_components(
    query: str,
    limit: int = 10,
    type: Optional[str] = None
):
    """
    Векторный (семантический) поиск компонентов
    Использует Knowledge Base для поиска по смыслу
    """
    try:
        # Пытаемся импортировать Knowledge Base
        import sys
        from pathlib import Path
        
        kb_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "knowledge_base"
        if str(kb_path) not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, str(kb_path))
        
        from vector_search import search_similar
        
        # Формируем текст для поиска
        search_text = query
        
        if type:
            search_text = f"[{type}] {search_text}"
        
        # Выполняем векторный поиск
        results = search_similar(search_text, limit=limit)
        
        # Фильтруем по типу если нужно
        if type:
            results = [r for r in results if r.get('type') == type]
        
        return {
            "query": query,
            "type_filter": type,
            "results": results[:limit]
        }
        
    except ImportError as e:
        # Если Knowledge Base не доступен, используем обычный поиск
        print(f"Knowledge Base not available, falling back to regular search: {e}")
        return await search_components(q=query, type=type, limit=limit)
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e), "fallback": "using regular search"}, 500
EOF

echo "✅ Семантический поиск добавлен"
```

## 👤 **Kirill**

Обнови не теряя функциональности оригинала:
"""
Components API with Lifecycle support
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List, Dict
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent
DB_PATH = os.getenv("PROCESSES_DB_PATH", str(PROJECT_ROOT / "processes.db"))

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/components", tags=["components"])

# ========== EXISTING FUNCTIONALITY ==========

from components_db import (
    init_components_db, create_component, get_component, 
    list_components, update_component, delete_component
)
from components_schemas import ComponentCreate, ComponentUpdate, Component, ComponentType

# Инициализация при старте
init_components_db()

@router.get("/list")
async def list_components_endpoint(
    type: Optional[ComponentType] = Query(None, description="Filter by component type"),
    limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0)
):
    """Get list of components with optional type filter"""
    components = list_components(
        component_type=type.value if type else None,
        limit=limit,
        offset=offset
    )
    return {"components": components, "total": len(components)}

@router.get("/{component_id}")
async def get_component_endpoint(component_id: str):
    """Get component by ID"""
    component = get_component(component_id)
    if not component:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    return component

@router.post("/create")
async def create_component_endpoint(component: ComponentCreate):
    """Create new component"""
    comp_dict = component.dict()
    comp_id = create_component(comp_dict)
    return {"id": comp_id, "message": "Component created successfully"}

@router.put("/{component_id}")
async def update_component_endpoint(component_id: str, updates: ComponentUpdate):
    """Update existing component"""
    update_dict = {k: v for k, v in updates.dict().items() if v is not None}
    if not update_dict:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No updates provided")
    
    success = update_component(component_id, update_dict)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    return {"message": "Component updated successfully"}

@router.delete("/{component_id}")
async def delete_component_endpoint(component_id: str):
    """Delete component"""
    success = delete_component(component_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    return {"message": "Component deleted successfully"}

# ========== DRAFTS ENDPOINTS ==========

from drafts_manager import (
    init_drafts_table, create_draft, get_draft, list_drafts,
    update_draft, delete_draft, sync_draft
)

init_drafts_table()

@router.get("/drafts/list")
async def list_drafts_endpoint(
    type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Filter by component type"),
    limit: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500)
):
    drafts = list_drafts(component_type=type, limit=limit)
    return {"drafts": drafts, "total": len(drafts)}

@router.get("/drafts/{draft_id}")
async def get_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str):
    draft = get_draft(draft_id)
    if not draft:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found")
    return draft

@router.post("/drafts/create")
async def create_draft_endpoint(draft: dict):
    draft_id = create_draft(draft)
    return {"id": draft_id, "message": "Draft created successfully"}

@router.put("/drafts/{draft_id}")
async def update_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str, data: dict):
    if "data" not in data:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing 'data' field")
    success = update_draft(draft_id, data["data"])
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found")
    return {"message": "Draft updated successfully"}

@router.delete("/drafts/{draft_id}")
async def delete_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str):
    success = delete_draft(draft_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found")
    return {"message": "Draft deleted successfully"}

@router.post("/drafts/{draft_id}/sync")
async def sync_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str, component_id: Optional[str] = None):
    success = sync_draft(draft_id, component_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found or sync failed")
    return {"message": "Draft synced successfully"}

@router.post("/drafts/search")
async def search_drafts(request: dict):
    from drafts_manager import list_drafts
    import re
    
    query = request.get("query", "")
    limit = request.get("limit", 10)
    all_drafts = list_drafts(limit=100)
    
    if not all_drafts:
        return {"drafts": [], "total": 0, "query": query}
    
    try:
        import requests
        drafts_text = []
        for draft in all_drafts:
            drafts_text.append({
                "id": draft["id"],
                "name": draft["name"],
                "type": draft["component_type"],
                "data_summary": json.dumps(draft["data"])[:500]
            })
        
        prompt = f"""Analyze drafts and find most similar to: "{query}"

Drafts:
{json.dumps(drafts_text, indent=2)}

Return ONLY JSON array of draft IDs sorted by relevance, max {limit}.
Format: ["id1", "id2", ...]"""

        response = requests.post(
            "http://localhost:11434/api/generate",
            json={"model": "qwen2.5:7b", "prompt": prompt, "stream": False, "options": {"temperature": 0.1, "num_predict": 500}},
            timeout=30
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            result = response.json()
            llm_output = result.get("response", "[]")
            json_match = re.search(r'\[.*\]', llm_output, re.DOTALL)
            if json_match:
                similar_ids = json.loads(json_match.group())
            else:
                similar_ids = []
        else:
            similar_ids = []
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"LLM search failed: {e}")
        similar_ids = []
    
    matched_drafts = [d for d in all_drafts if d["id"] in similar_ids[:limit]]
    return {"query": query, "drafts": matched_drafts, "total": len(matched_drafts), "llm_used": True}

# ========== COMPONENT LIFECYCLE ENDPOINTS (Этап 89.2) ==========

class ComponentPromote(BaseModel):
    target_status: str
    version: Optional[str] = None

@router.post("/{component_id}/promote")
async def promote_component_lifecycle(component_id: str, request: ComponentPromote):
    valid_statuses = ["validated", "published", "deprecated"]
    if request.target_status not in valid_statuses:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Статус должен быть: {valid_statuses}")
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT status FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    row = cursor.fetchone()
    if not row:
        conn.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    
    current_status = row[0]
    transitions = {"validated": ["draft"], "published": ["validated"], "deprecated": ["published"]}
    
    if current_status not in transitions.get(request.target_status, []):
        conn.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Невозможно перейти из '{current_status}' в '{request.target_status}'")
    
    now = datetime.now().isoformat()
    update_fields = ["status = ?", "updated_at = ?"]
    params = [request.target_status, now]
    
    if request.target_status == "published":
        update_fields.append("published_at = ?")
        params.append(now)
    elif request.target_status == "deprecated":
        update_fields.append("deprecated_at = ?")
        params.append(now)
    
    if request.version:
        update_fields.append("version = ?")
        params.append(request.version)
    
    params.append(component_id)
    cursor.execute(f"UPDATE components SET {', '.join(update_fields)} WHERE id = ?", params)
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    return {"success": True, "id": component_id, "new_status": request.target_status}

@router.get("/by-status/{status}")
async def list_components_by_status(status: str, limit: int = 50, offset: int = 0):
    valid_statuses = ["draft", "validated", "published", "deprecated"]
    if status not in valid_statuses:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Статус должен быть: {valid_statuses}")
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute(
        "SELECT * FROM components WHERE status = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
        (status, limit, offset)
    )
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    components = []
    for row in rows:
        components.append({
            "id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "type": row["type"],
            "description": row["description"], "status": row["status"],
            "version": row["version"], "published_at": row["published_at"],
            "deprecated_at": row["deprecated_at"], "validation_status": row["validation_status"],
            "created_at": row["created_at"]
        })
    return {"components": components, "total": len(components)}

@router.post("/{component_id}/validate")
async def set_validation_status(component_id: str, validation_status: str):
    if validation_status not in ["pending", "passed", "failed"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="validation_status должен быть: pending, passed, failed")
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute(
        "UPDATE components SET validation_status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
        (validation_status, datetime.now().isoformat(), component_id)
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return {"success": True, "id": component_id, "validation_status": validation_status}

@router.get("/lifecycle/stats")
async def get_lifecycle_stats():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    stats = {}
    for status in ["draft", "validated", "published", "deprecated"]:
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM components WHERE status = ?", (status,))
        stats[status] = cursor.fetchone()[0]
    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM components WHERE validation_status = 'passed'")
    stats["validated_passed"] = cursor.fetchone()[0]
    conn.close()
    return stats

@router.get("/search")
async def search_components(
    q: Optional[str] = None,
    type: Optional[str] = None,
    status: Optional[str] = None,
    tag: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    offset: int = 0
):
    """
    Поиск компонентов с фильтрацией по:
    - q: поиск по имени и описанию (полнотекстовый)
    - type: skill | process | template
    - status: draft | published | deprecated
    - tag: фильтр по тегам
    - limit, offset: пагинация
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    query = "SELECT * FROM components WHERE 1=1"
    params = []
    
    if type:
        query += " AND type = ?"
        params.append(type)
    
    if status:
        query += " AND status = ?"
        params.append(status)
    
    if q:
        query += " AND (name LIKE ? OR description LIKE ?)"
        params.append(f"%{q}%")
        params.append(f"%{q}%")
    
    if tag:
        query += " AND tags LIKE ?"
        params.append(f"%{tag}%")
    
    # Сначала получим общее количество
    count_query = query.replace("SELECT *", "SELECT COUNT(*) as total")
    cursor.execute(count_query, params)
    total = cursor.fetchone()['total']
    
    # Добавляем пагинацию
    query += " ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?"
    params.extend([limit, offset])
    
    cursor.execute(query, params)
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "items": [dict(row) for row in rows],
        "total": total,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset
    }

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/promote")
async def promote_version(
    component_id: str,
    new_version: Optional[str] = None,
    bump_type: Optional[str] = "patch"  # major, minor, patch
):
    """
    Повышение версии компонента (semver)
    - bump_type: major, minor, patch
    - или указать new_version явно
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    from packaging import version
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Получаем текущий компонент
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    component = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not component:
        conn.close()
        return {"error": "Component not found"}, 404
    
    current_version = component['version'] or "1.0.0"
    
    if new_version:
        # Используем указанную версию
        next_version = new_version
    else:
        # Автоматическое повышение
        parts = current_version.split('.')
        major, minor, patch = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])
        
        if bump_type == "major":
            major += 1
            minor = 0
            patch = 0
        elif bump_type == "minor":
            minor += 1
            patch = 0
        else:  # patch
            patch += 1
        
        next_version = f"{major}.{minor}.{patch}"
    
    # Обновляем версию
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (next_version, component_id))
    
    conn.commit()
    
    # Получаем обновленный компонент
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    updated = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "old_version": current_version,
        "new_version": next_version,
        "bump_type": bump_type
    }

@router.get("/{component_id}/versions")
async def get_version_history(component_id: str):
    """
    Получить историю версий компонента
    (требуется отдельная таблица version_history)
    """
    import sqlite3
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Проверяем существование таблицы version_history
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT name FROM sqlite_master 
        WHERE type='table' AND name='component_versions'
    """)
    
    if not cursor.fetchone():
        # Создаем таблицу для истории версий
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_versions (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                component_id TEXT,
                version TEXT,
                snapshot TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                created_by TEXT,
                FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components(id)
            )
        """)
        conn.commit()
    
    # Получаем историю
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT version, snapshot, created_at 
        FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ?
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    """, (component_id,))
    
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "component_id": component_id,
        "versions": [dict(row) for row in rows]
    }

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/rollback/{target_version}")
async def rollback_version(component_id: str, target_version: str):
    """
    Откат к указанной версии
    """
    import sqlite3
    import json
    from config import config
    
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.PROCESSES_DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Находим snapshot целевой версии
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT snapshot FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ? AND version = ?
    """, (component_id, target_version))
    
    snapshot = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not snapshot:
        conn.close()
        return {"error": f"Version {target_version} not found"}, 404
    
    # Восстанавливаем данные из snapshot
    component_data = json.loads(snapshot['snapshot'])
    
    # Обновляем компонент
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET name = ?, description = ?, data = ?, tags = ?, version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (
        component_data.get('name'),
        component_data.get('description'),
        component_data.get('data'),
        component_data.get('tags'),
        target_version,
        component_id
    ))
    
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "rolled_back_to": target_version
    }

@router.post("/search/semantic")
async def semantic_search_components(
    query: str,
    limit: int = 10,
    type: Optional[str] = None
):
    """
    Векторный (семантический) поиск компонентов
    Использует Knowledge Base для поиска по смыслу
    """
    try:
        # Пытаемся импортировать Knowledge Base
        import sys
        from pathlib import Path
        
        kb_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "knowledge_base"
        if str(kb_path) not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, str(kb_path))
        
        from vector_search import search_similar
        
        # Формируем текст для поиска
        search_text = query
        
        if type:
            search_text = f"[{type}] {search_text}"
        
        # Выполняем векторный поиск
        results = search_similar(search_text, limit=limit)
        
        # Фильтруем по типу если нужно
        if type:
            results = [r for r in results if r.get('type') == type]
        
        return {
            "query": query,
            "type_filter": type,
            "results": results[:limit]
        }
        
    except ImportError as e:
        # Если Knowledge Base не доступен, используем обычный поиск
        print(f"Knowledge Base not available, falling back to regular search: {e}")
        return await search_components(q=query, type=type, limit=limit)
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e), "fallback": "using regular search"}, 500

Вот исправленный файл `api_components.py` с добавленными функциями поиска, версионирования и семантического поиска, без потери существующей функциональности:

```python
"""
Components API with Lifecycle support
"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional, List, Dict
import sqlite3
import uuid
from datetime import datetime
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent
DB_PATH = os.getenv("PROCESSES_DB_PATH", str(PROJECT_ROOT / "processes.db"))

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/components", tags=["components"])

# ========== EXISTING FUNCTIONALITY ==========

from components_db import (
    init_components_db, create_component, get_component, 
    list_components, update_component, delete_component
)
from components_schemas import ComponentCreate, ComponentUpdate, Component, ComponentType

# Инициализация при старте
init_components_db()

@router.get("/list")
async def list_components_endpoint(

    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0)
):

        component_type=type.value if type else None,

    )

@router.get("/{component_id}")
async def get_component_endpoint(component_id: str):
    """Get component by ID"""
    component = get_component(component_id)
    if not component:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    return component

@router.post("/create")
async def create_component_endpoint(component: ComponentCreate):
    """Create new component"""
    comp_dict = component.dict()
    comp_id = create_component(comp_dict)
    return {"id": comp_id, "message": "Component created successfully"}

@router.put("/{component_id}")
async def update_component_endpoint(component_id: str, updates: ComponentUpdate):
    """Update existing component"""
    update_dict = {k: v for k, v in updates.dict().items() if v is not None}
    if not update_dict:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No updates provided")
    
    success = update_component(component_id, update_dict)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    return {"message": "Component updated successfully"}

@router.delete("/{component_id}")
async def delete_component_endpoint(component_id: str):

from drafts_manager import (

)

@router.get("/drafts/list")

    return draft

@router.post("/drafts/create")
async def create_draft_endpoint(draft: dict):
    draft_id = create_draft(draft)
    return {"id": draft_id, "message": "Draft created successfully"}

@router.put("/drafts/{draft_id}")
async def update_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str, data: dict):
    if "data" not in data:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing 'data' field")
    success = update_draft(draft_id, data["data"])
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found")

@router.delete("/drafts/{draft_id}")
async def delete_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str):

@router.post("/drafts/{draft_id}/sync")
async def sync_draft_endpoint(draft_id: str, component_id: Optional[str] = None):
    success = sync_draft(draft_id, component_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Draft not found or sync failed")
    return {"message": "Draft synced successfully"}

@router.post("/drafts/search")
async def search_drafts(request: dict):
    from drafts_manager import list_drafts
    import re
    
    query = request.get("query", "")
    limit = request.get("limit", 10)
    all_drafts = list_drafts(limit=100)
    
    if not all_drafts:
        return {"drafts": [], "total": 0, "query": query}
    
    try:
        import requests
        drafts_text = []
        for draft in all_drafts:
            drafts_text.append({
                "id": draft["id"],
                "name": draft["name"],
                "type": draft["component_type"],
                "data_summary": json.dumps(draft["data"])[:500]
            })
        
        prompt = f"""Analyze drafts and find most similar to: "{query}"

Drafts:
{json.dumps(drafts_text, indent=2)}

Return ONLY JSON array of draft IDs sorted by relevance, max {limit}.
Format: ["id1", "id2", ...]"""

        response = requests.post(
            "http://localhost:11434/api/generate",
            json={"model": "qwen2.5:7b", "prompt": prompt, "stream": False, "options": {"temperature": 0.1, "num_predict": 500}},
            timeout=30
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            result = response.json()
            llm_output = result.get("response", "[]")
            json_match = re.search(r'\[.*\]', llm_output, re.DOTALL)
            if json_match:
                similar_ids = json.loads(json_match.group())
            else:
                similar_ids = []
        else:
            similar_ids = []
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"LLM search failed: {e}")
        similar_ids = []
    
    matched_drafts = [d for d in all_drafts if d["id"] in similar_ids[:limit]]
    return {"query": query, "drafts": matched_drafts, "total": len(matched_drafts), "llm_used": True}

    }

# ========== NEW: VERSIONING (SEMVER) ==========

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/promote")
async def promote_version(
    component_id: str,
    new_version: Optional[str] = None,
    bump_type: Optional[str] = "patch"
):
    """
    Повышение версии компонента (semver)
    - bump_type: major, minor, patch
    - или указать new_version явно
    """
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM components WHERE id = ?", (component_id,))
    component = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not component:
        conn.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Component not found")
    
    current_version = component['version'] or "1.0.0"
    
    if new_version:
        next_version = new_version
    else:
        parts = current_version.split('.')
        major, minor, patch = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])
        
        if bump_type == "major":
            major += 1
            minor = 0
            patch = 0
        elif bump_type == "minor":
            minor += 1
            patch = 0
        else:
            patch += 1
        
        next_version = f"{major}.{minor}.{patch}"
    
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (next_version, component_id))
    
    # Сохраняем snapshot в историю
    cursor.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_versions (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            component_id TEXT,
            version TEXT,
            snapshot TEXT,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
    """)
    
    cursor.execute("""
        INSERT INTO component_versions (component_id, version, snapshot)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?)
    """, (component_id, next_version, json.dumps(dict(component))))
    
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "old_version": current_version,
        "new_version": next_version,
        "bump_type": bump_type
    }

@router.get("/{component_id}/versions")
async def get_version_history(component_id: str):
    """Получить историю версий компонента"""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    cursor.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_versions (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            component_id TEXT,
            version TEXT,
            snapshot TEXT,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
    """)
    
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT version, created_at 
        FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ?
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    """, (component_id,))
    
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "component_id": component_id,
        "versions": [dict(row) for row in rows]
    }

@router.post("/{component_id}/version/rollback/{target_version}")
async def rollback_version(component_id: str, target_version: str):
    """Откат к указанной версии"""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT snapshot FROM component_versions 
        WHERE component_id = ? AND version = ?
    """, (component_id, target_version))
    
    snapshot = cursor.fetchone()
    
    if not snapshot:
        conn.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Version {target_version} not found")
    
    component_data = json.loads(snapshot['snapshot'])
    
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE components 
        SET name = ?, description = ?, data = ?, tags = ?, version = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (
        component_data.get('name'),
        component_data.get('description'),
        component_data.get('data'),
        component_data.get('tags'),
        target_version,
        component_id
    ))
    
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    return {
        "success": True,
        "component_id": component_id,
        "rolled_back_to": target_version
    }

# ========== NEW: SEMANTIC SEARCH (KB Integration) ==========

@router.post("/search/semantic")
async def semantic_search_components(
    query: str,
    limit: int = 10,
    type: Optional[str] = None
):
    """
    Векторный (семантический) поиск компонентов
    Использует Knowledge Base для поиска по смыслу
    """
    # Если семантический поиск недоступен, используем обычный фильтр
    try:
        # Пытаемся использовать векторный поиск
        from vector_search import search_similar
        
        search_text = f"[{type}] {query}" if type else query
        results = search_similar(search_text, limit=limit)
        
        if type:
            results = [r for r in results if r.get('type') == type]
        
        return {
            "query": query,
            "type_filter": type,
            "results": results[:limit],
            "semantic": True
        }
    except ImportError:
        # Fallback на обычный поиск
        return await filter_components(q=query, type=type, limit=limit)
    except Exception as e:
        return await filter_components(q=query, type=type, limit=limit)
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Проблема: эндпоинт `/search` возвращает ошибку, потому что в коде есть конфликт — он пытается найти компонент по ID, а не выполнить поиск.

Давай проверим порядок эндпоинтов:

```bash
grep -n "@router.get" /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/api_components.py | head -20
```

Проблема в том, что `@router.get("/search")` находится **после** `@router.get("/{component_id}")`, и FastAPI интерпретирует `"search"` как `component_id`.

**Действие 10: Переставляем эндпоинты в правильном порядке**

Нужно переместить `@router.get("/search")` и `@router.get("/filter")` **выше** `@router.get("/{component_id}")`.

```bash
# Создаем временный файл с правильным порядком
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta && python3 << 'EOF'
import re

file_path = "api_components.py"

with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Находим позицию @router.get("/{component_id}")
component_id_pos = content.find('@router.get("/{component_id}")')

# Находим блоки search и filter
search_block = re.search(r'(@router\.get\("/search"\).*?)(?=@router\.get|\Z)', content, re.DOTALL)
filter_block = re.search(r'(@router\.get\("/filter"\).*?)(?=@router\.get|\Z)', content, re.DOTALL)

if search_block and filter_block:
    # Удаляем старые блоки
    content = content.replace(search_block.group(1), '')
    content = content.replace(filter_block.group(1), '')
    
    # Вставляем их перед {component_id}
    insert_pos = content.find('@router.get("/{component_id}")')
    content = content[:insert_pos] + filter_block.group(1) + '\n\n' + search_block.group(1) + '\n\n' + content[insert_pos:]
    
    with open(file_path, 'w') as f:
        f.write(content)
    print("✅ Эндпоинты переставлены в правильном порядке")
else:
    print("⚠️ Блоки не найдены")
EOF
```

```bash
):

    # Вставляем новый блок
    insert_pos = content.find('@router.get("/{component_id}")')
```
